<a id="overview"></a>

## Overview

The SP Trading API is the machine interface of SP Wallet: market data, the portfolio, positions, orders, strategies and agents — 46 operations under `/api/v1/trading`, one owner, keys he creates with his passkey.

| | |
|---|---|
| Base URL | `https://wallet.spboucher.ai/api/v1/trading` |
| Format | JSON in, JSON out, UTF-8. Request bodies are strict: an unknown field is refused (400 `bad_request`) |
| Authentication | `Authorization: Bearer spw_live_…` |
| Contract | OpenAPI 3.1 at [`/api/v1/trading/openapi.json`](/api/v1/trading/openapi.json), generated from the same table the server registers its routes from |
| This reference | [`/api/v1/trading/docs`](/api/v1/trading/docs) (HTML), [`/api/v1/trading/docs.md`](/api/v1/trading/docs.md) (Markdown) — both need a key or the owner session, like every path of the API |
| SDKs | TypeScript `@sp-wallet/trading-sdk` (`packages/trading-sdk`), Python `sp-wallet-python` (`sdk/python`) |
| Live market data | websocket `/api/v1/trading/stream` |

Three rules run through everything:

- **Money is a string.** Prices, sizes, amounts and rates are decimal strings; send strings, parse with a decimal type.
- **Paper and live are never mixed.** Every row that could be either carries `mode`; no figure adds the two.
- **A permission is not an authorization to trade.** A scope lets a key *call* an operation. What bounds an order is a trading mandate the owner approved, checked on every order — and, for live orders, enforced by the isolated signer.

<a id="getting-started"></a>

## Getting started in five minutes

Five minutes from nothing to a simulated order. Everything below was run against a real server; the answers shown are recorded.

### 1. Create a key

In the wallet app: **Settings → Developer / API → Keys → New key**.

- **Name** — anything (`my bot`).
- **Expiry** — required, at most 365 days.
- **Permissions** — for reading: `wallet:read`, `markets:read`, `orders:read`, `positions:read`. For the paper order of step 3 add `orders:prepare`, `orders:execute`, `orders:cancel` and `positions:manage`.
- Approve with your passkey. The secret (`spw_live_spk_…`) is shown **once**: the server keeps only a hash of it.

A key opens `/api/v1/trading/` and nothing else — never the recovery phrase, the signer, the security settings or the keys themselves.

### 2. First call

```bash
export SP_WALLET_BASE_URL=https://wallet.spboucher.ai
export SP_WALLET_API_KEY=spw_live_spk_0123456789ab_EXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLE0   # yours, shown once

curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets?venue=hyperliquid&q=BTC&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
import { SPWallet } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

const client = new SPWallet();   // reads SP_WALLET_API_KEY and SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
const { markets } = await client.listMarkets({ venue: 'hyperliquid', q: 'BTC', limit: 3 });
console.log(markets[0].id, markets[0].mark);
```

```python
from sp_wallet import SPWallet

client = SPWallet()   # reads SP_WALLET_API_KEY and SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
markets = client.list_markets(venue="hyperliquid", q="BTC", limit=3).markets
print(markets[0].id, markets[0].mark)
```

```json
{
  "markets": [
    {
      "id": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "kind": "perpetual",
      "symbol": "BTC",
      "base": "BTC",
      "quote": "USD",
      "status": "active",
      "mark": "64432",
      "index": "64460.7",
      "funding": "0.0000089721",
      "fundingIntervalSec": 3600,
      "volume24h": "3621656019.2002997398",
      "openInterest": "37680.57528",
      "maxLeverage": "40",
      "tickSize": "0.1",
      "lotSize": "0.00001",
      "collateral": "USDC",
      "restriction": null,
      "ts": 1794953721101,
      "stale": false,
      "source": "venue"
    }
  ],
  "total": 1,
  "truncated": false,
  "errors": [],
  "ts": 1794953721103
}
```

Prices are **strings** (`"mark": "64432"`): never parse them as floats. `ts` is milliseconds since the epoch.

The SDKs are in the repository and are not published:

```bash
# TypeScript (not on npm: built from the repository)
node packages/trading-sdk/scripts/build.mts
npm install /path/to/sp-wallet/packages/trading-sdk

# Python (not on PyPI)
pip install /path/to/sp-wallet/sdk/python
```

### 3. First paper order

An order placed with a key needs three things, which only the owner can set up:

1. a **paper account** on Hyperliquid — app: **Futures → Accounts**;
2. a **paper trading mandate** for the key — app: **Settings → Risk → Trading mandates → New** (principal: your API key; mode: paper; the account; the instruments, e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`; the limits). A paper mandate signs nothing;
3. the mandate **bound to the key**. Without it, `orders:execute` is inert and the API answers:

```json
{
  "error": "mandate_required",
  "message": "the scope orders:execute is inert on this key: no trading mandate is bound to it"
}
```

> **Gap in the app.** The Developer screen has a "Mandate id" field only when a key is *created*, and a mandate can only be created for a key that already exists. Binding a mandate to an existing key is the owner route pair `POST /api/v1/developer/keys/{id}/update/prepare` `{ "changes": { "mandateId": "…" } }` then `POST /api/v1/developer/keys/{id}/update` `{ intentId, assertion }` (a passkey assertion; binding widens the key). No screen calls it yet.

Then:

```bash
# 1. what would happen — creates nothing
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/preview" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'

# 2. place it — one Idempotency-Key per logical order
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -H "Idempotency-Key: bot-2026-11-17-0001" -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'

# 3. read it, then close the position
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/positions?mode=paper" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
import { SPWallet } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

const client = new SPWallet();
const order = { mode: 'paper', instrument: 'hyperliquid:BTC', side: 'buy', type: 'market', notional: '100' } as const;

const preview = await client.previewOrder(order);              // creates nothing
if (!preview.wouldExecute) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(preview.refusals));

const placed = await client.createOrder(order, { idempotencyKey: 'bot-2026-11-17-0001' });
const done = await client.waitForOrder(placed.order.id);       // FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED or FAILED
console.log(done.order.status, done.order.filled, done.order.avgFillPrice);

const { positions } = await client.getPositions({ mode: 'paper' });
await client.closePosition(positions[0].id);                   // strictly reduce-only
```

```python
from sp_wallet import SPWallet

client = SPWallet()
order = dict(mode="paper", instrument="hyperliquid:BTC", side="buy", order_type="market", notional="100")

preview = client.preview_order(**order)                        # creates nothing
if not preview.would_execute:
    raise SystemExit(preview.refusals)

placed = client.create_order(**order, idempotency_key="bot-2026-11-17-0001")
done = client.wait_for_order(placed.order.id)                  # FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED or FAILED
print(done.order.status, done.order.filled, done.order.avg_fill_price)

positions = client.get_positions(mode="paper").positions
client.close_position(positions[0].id)                         # strictly reduce-only
```

The answer of step 2 — a paper market order is filled at once, against the real Hyperliquid order book, by the wallet's paper engine. Nothing is signed and nothing reaches a venue:

```json
{
  "order": {
    "id": "0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261",
    "orderId": "0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261",
    "mode": "paper",
    "venue": "hyperliquid",
    "network": "mainnet",
    "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
    "clientId": "0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261",
    "clientOrderId": "0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261",
    "venueOrderId": "paper-o-1",
    "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
    "side": "buy",
    "type": "market",
    "size": "0.00155",
    "price": "64754",
    "triggerPrice": null,
    "reduceOnly": false,
    "tif": "ioc",
    "status": "FILLED",
    "final": true,
    "reasonCode": "VENUE_FILLED",
    "reason": null,
    "rejectCode": null,
    "engineStatus": "filled",
    "filled": "0.00155",
    "avgFillPrice": "64432",
    "acknowledged": true,
    "cancelRequested": false,
    "origin": "trading_api",
    "principal": {
      "kind": "api_key",
      "id": "spk_0123456789ab"
    },
    "mandateId": "84f954ea-0c25-4985-bc19-901d510964d7",
    "engine": "paper",
    "engineRef": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261",
    "hash": "1d3481aaee13d8c193c215ba06f786da1cc978c805d9d2f57a102564ddf5c386",
    "notionalUsd": "100.3687",
    "projectionError": null,
    "version": 4,
    "createdAt": 1794953729578,
    "updatedAt": 1794953729602
  },
  "idempotent": false,
  "approval": null
}
```

The same call with the same `Idempotency-Key` returns the same order (header `Idempotent-Replay: true`): a retry can never place it twice.

If the wallet has several paper accounts on the venue (a perp agent gets its own when it is created), the API asks which: 409 `account_required`. Add `"accountId"` to the order — the ids are in `GET /portfolio` (`accounts`).

<a id="demarrage-rapide"></a>

## Démarrage rapide (français)

Cinq minutes pour passer de rien à un ordre simulé. Les exemples sont ceux de la section anglaise : les routes, les champs et les réponses sont les mêmes.

### 1. Créer une clé

Dans l'application : **Réglages → Développeur / API → Clés → Nouvelle clé**.

- **Nom** — libre (`mon robot`).
- **Expiration** — obligatoire, au plus 365 jours.
- **Permissions** — pour lire : `wallet:read`, `markets:read`, `orders:read`, `positions:read`. Pour l'ordre simulé de l'étape 3, ajoutez `orders:prepare`, `orders:execute`, `orders:cancel` et `positions:manage`.
- Approuvez avec votre clé d'accès. Le secret (`spw_live_spk_…`) n'est affiché **qu'une fois** : le serveur n'en garde qu'une empreinte.

Une clé n'ouvre que `/api/v1/trading/` — jamais la phrase de récupération, le signataire, la sécurité ni les clés elles-mêmes.

### 2. Premier appel

```bash
export SP_WALLET_BASE_URL=https://wallet.spboucher.ai
export SP_WALLET_API_KEY=spw_live_spk_0123456789ab_EXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLE0   # yours, shown once

curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets?venue=hyperliquid&q=BTC&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
import { SPWallet } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

const client = new SPWallet();   // reads SP_WALLET_API_KEY and SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
const { markets } = await client.listMarkets({ venue: 'hyperliquid', q: 'BTC', limit: 3 });
console.log(markets[0].id, markets[0].mark);
```

```python
from sp_wallet import SPWallet

client = SPWallet()   # reads SP_WALLET_API_KEY and SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
markets = client.list_markets(venue="hyperliquid", q="BTC", limit=3).markets
print(markets[0].id, markets[0].mark)
```

Les prix sont des **chaînes** (`"mark": "64432"`) : ne les lisez jamais comme des flottants. `ts` est en millisecondes depuis l'époque Unix.

### 3. Premier ordre simulé (paper)

Un ordre passé avec une clé exige trois choses, que seul le propriétaire peut mettre en place :

1. un **compte simulé** sur Hyperliquid — application : **Contrats à terme → Comptes** ;
2. un **mandat de négociation simulé** pour la clé — application : **Réglages → Risque → Mandats de négociation → Nouveau** (mandant : votre clé d'API ; mode : simulé ; le compte ; les instruments, par exemple `hyperliquid:BTC` ; les limites). Un mandat simulé ne signe rien ;
3. le mandat **lié à la clé**. Sans lui, `orders:execute` est inerte et l'API répond `403 mandate_required`.

> **Lacune de l'application.** L'écran Développeur n'offre le champ « Identifiant du mandat » qu'à la *création* d'une clé, et un mandat ne peut être créé que pour une clé qui existe déjà. Lier un mandat à une clé existante passe par les deux routes du propriétaire `POST /api/v1/developer/keys/{id}/update/prepare` puis `POST /api/v1/developer/keys/{id}/update` (assertion de la clé d'accès). Aucun écran ne les appelle pour l'instant.

Ensuite :

```bash
# 1. what would happen — creates nothing
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/preview" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'

# 2. place it — one Idempotency-Key per logical order
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -H "Idempotency-Key: bot-2026-11-17-0001" -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'

# 3. read it, then close the position
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/positions?mode=paper" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
import { SPWallet } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

const client = new SPWallet();
const order = { mode: 'paper', instrument: 'hyperliquid:BTC', side: 'buy', type: 'market', notional: '100' } as const;

const preview = await client.previewOrder(order);              // creates nothing
if (!preview.wouldExecute) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(preview.refusals));

const placed = await client.createOrder(order, { idempotencyKey: 'bot-2026-11-17-0001' });
const done = await client.waitForOrder(placed.order.id);       // FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED or FAILED
console.log(done.order.status, done.order.filled, done.order.avgFillPrice);

const { positions } = await client.getPositions({ mode: 'paper' });
await client.closePosition(positions[0].id);                   // strictly reduce-only
```

```python
from sp_wallet import SPWallet

client = SPWallet()
order = dict(mode="paper", instrument="hyperliquid:BTC", side="buy", order_type="market", notional="100")

preview = client.preview_order(**order)                        # creates nothing
if not preview.would_execute:
    raise SystemExit(preview.refusals)

placed = client.create_order(**order, idempotency_key="bot-2026-11-17-0001")
done = client.wait_for_order(placed.order.id)                  # FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED or FAILED
print(done.order.status, done.order.filled, done.order.avg_fill_price)

positions = client.get_positions(mode="paper").positions
client.close_position(positions[0].id)                         # strictly reduce-only
```

Un ordre au marché simulé est exécuté tout de suite par le moteur de simulation du portefeuille, contre le vrai carnet d'ordres d'Hyperliquid. Rien n'est signé, rien ne part vers une plateforme. Le même appel avec le même `Idempotency-Key` renvoie le même ordre : une relance ne peut pas le passer deux fois.

### 4. Ordres réels

Un ordre réel (`"mode": "live"`) passe par Hyperliquid seulement, sous un mandat réel approuvé avec la clé d'accès et appliqué par le signataire isolé, et seulement si le serveur a `SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION=on`. Voir [Paper and live](#paper-live) et [Why an order is refused](#refusals).

<a id="authentication"></a>

## Authentication and permissions

Every request carries the key in one header. No cookie, no CSRF header, no query parameter — a key in a URL opens nothing.

```http
GET /api/v1/trading/markets HTTP/1.1
Host: wallet.spboucher.ai
Authorization: Bearer spw_live_spk_0123456789ab_EXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLE0
```

The token is `spw_live_<key id>_<secret>`. The key id (`spk_` + 12 hex) is public and appears in lists, logs and webhooks; the secret is 32 random bytes shown once. A missing, malformed, unknown, expired or revoked key gets one answer:

```json
{
  "error": "unauthenticated",
  "message": "authentication required"
}
```

10 failed authentications in a minute from one address lock that address out for the rest of the minute (429 `auth_throttled`). A request that carries a key **and** the session cookie is refused (403 `mixed_credentials`). Outside `/api/v1/trading/` a key is refused before it is even looked up (403 `key_path_forbidden`).

A key answer carries `X-Request-Id`: quote it when something needs investigating; it is in the usage log (Developer → Usage, kept 90 days by default).

### Permissions (scopes)

| Scope | Opens | Execution scope |
|---|---|---|
| `wallet:read` | Balances, the portfolio, activity, the wallet's NFTs. | — |
| `markets:read` | Markets, order books, candles, the venue comparator, private markets, NFT market data, and the websocket stream. | — |
| `orders:read` | Orders and their fills. | — |
| `orders:prepare` | Preview an order; dry-run a command. Creates nothing. | — |
| `orders:execute` | Place an order. Execution scope: inert without a mandate bound to the key. | yes |
| `orders:cancel` | Cancel the key's own orders. Needs no mandate: a cancel only removes risk. | yes |
| `positions:read` | Positions and the risk state. | — |
| `positions:manage` | Close or reduce a position (needs a mandate); engage the kill switch (needs none). | yes |
| `strategies:read` | The strategy library and the versions pinned to agents. | — |
| `strategies:manage` | Register, backtest, start and stop strategy versions. Research and paper sign nothing. | — |
| `agents:read` | Agents, their signals, journal, orders, performance, jobs. | — |
| `agents:manage` | Create, start, pause, stop and backtest agents. Going live needs the agent's own owner-approved mandate. | — |

Every operation names the one scope it needs. A key without it gets:

```json
{
  "error": "insufficient_scope",
  "message": "this key lacks the scope wallet:read"
}
```

The three **execution scopes** are inert until a trading mandate is bound to the key (403 `mandate_required`), with two exceptions that can only remove risk: cancelling the key's own order, and engaging the kill switch.

### What else a key carries

- **Expiry** — required, at most 365 days. At most 50 keys.
- **Rate limit** — 120 calls a minute by default, at most 600.
- **Restrictions** — optional allow-lists of venues, instruments, networks and source IP addresses / CIDR blocks. Outside them: 403 `restricted` or `ip_not_allowed`.
- **Limits** — optional notional per order and per day, in USD (403 `key_limit_exceeded`).
- **Mandate** — the id of the trading mandate bound to it, or none.

Narrowing a key needs the owner's session; anything that widens it — a scope, a later expiry, a looser restriction, a mandate — needs the passkey. **Rotating** a key creates a new id with the same settings and revokes the old one at once; the mandate named the old id, so the new key has no mandate until one is approved and bound again.

<a id="formats"></a>

## Decimals, timestamps, identifiers

### Decimals

Anything that carries money or a quantity is a decimal **string**: `"64432"`, `"0.00155"`, `"0.0000089721"`. In a request the form is `^\d+(\.\d+)?$` — no sign, no exponent, no thousands separator. A size must be a multiple of the instrument's `lotSize` and a price a multiple of its `tickSize` (both in `GET /markets/{symbol}`); the simplest way to respect the lot is to send `notional` and let the server compute the size.

Counts and settings are JSON numbers (`limit`, `slippageBps`, `fundingIntervalSec`, `decimals`).

### Timestamps

Two forms, by family — the field name tells which:

- **milliseconds since the Unix epoch**, a JSON integer: `ts`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`, `expiresAt`, `at` of a switch — markets, orders, positions, agents, risk;
- **ISO 8601 UTC strings**: `asOf` (portfolio, balances, NFTs), `at` of an activity item, `priceAt`, `fetchedAt`, the `ts` of a webhook envelope.

The optional request header `X-SPW-Timestamp` is Unix **seconds**.

### Freshness

Market rows carry `ts` (when the source produced the value) and `stale` (older than its freshness bound, or the source could not be refreshed). A stale row is shown, never hidden: decide on `stale`. Lists that read several sources carry `errors: [{ source, message }]` for the ones that failed, and answer 503 only when none answered.

### Identifiers

| Thing | Form | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Instrument / market | `venue:symbol` | `hyperliquid:BTC`, `gmx:BTC/USD [BTC-USDC]`, `dydx:BTC-USD`, `spot:ETH` |
| Order | opaque string | `0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261` |
| Position | `mode:venue:account:instrument` — URL-encode it in a path | `paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC` |
| Key | `spk_` + 12 hex | `spk_0123456789ab` |
| Agent, job, strategy version, mandate, account | UUID | `b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b` |

A bare symbol (`BTC`) is accepted where a market is named, when it is not ambiguous; otherwise:

```json
{
  "error": "ambiguous_symbol",
  "message": "'BTC' exists on several venues: name one of the candidates as venue:symbol",
  "candidates": [
    "dydx:BTC-USD",
    "edgex:BTCUSDC"
  ]
}
```

<a id="instruments"></a>

## Instruments and venues

What can be read and what can be traded are different lists.

| Venue | Market data | Paper orders (API) | Live orders (API key) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hyperliquid` | yes | yes | **yes** — the perpetuals of Hyperliquid's own dex (179 active out of 235 listed when this page was recorded, 2026-11-17), ids `hyperliquid:BTC`, `hyperliquid:ETH`, … |
| `gmx` | yes (pool figures, no order book) | no — the paper engine needs a book (422 `paper_unsupported`) | no — every GMX order is a wallet transaction the owner approves in the app (403 `gmx_requires_owner_passkey`) |
| `dydx` | yes | no | no — market data only (403 `venue_restricted`) |
| `edgex` | yes | no | no — market data only (403 `venue_restricted`) |
| `spot` | yes (bars, no order book) | no | no — a key cannot place a spot order (403 `spot_execution_requires_agent_vault`); an owner's spot order is a swap approved with the passkey |

`GET /markets?venue=hyperliquid` lists the instruments with what an order must respect:

```json
{
  "id": "hyperliquid:BTC",
  "venue": "hyperliquid",
  "kind": "perpetual",
  "symbol": "BTC",
  "base": "BTC",
  "quote": "USD",
  "status": "active",
  "mark": "64432",
  "index": "64460.7",
  "funding": "0.0000089721",
  "fundingIntervalSec": 3600,
  "volume24h": "3621656019.2002997398",
  "openInterest": "37680.57528",
  "maxLeverage": "40",
  "tickSize": "0.1",
  "lotSize": "0.00001",
  "collateral": "USDC",
  "restriction": null,
  "ts": 1794953721101,
  "stale": false,
  "source": "venue"
}
```

**Private markets** (`GET /private-markets`: synthetic contracts on private companies, on Hyperliquid builder dexes and edgeX) are display only — `tradable` is always `false`. Ids have three parts there: `hyperliquid:io:ANTH`.

Candle intervals: `1m`, `5m`, `15m`, `1h`, `4h`, `1d` (spot market data stores fewer: 422 `unsupported_interval`).

<a id="paper-live"></a>

## Paper and live, the execution lock

Every order names its `mode`.

| | `paper` | `live` |
|---|---|---|
| What executes | the wallet's paper engine, against the venue's real public order book | the venue (Hyperliquid) |
| Money | simulated collateral of a paper account | the collateral of an authorized venue account |
| Signature | none | the isolated signer signs under the mandate it stores |
| Mandate (key) | a paper mandate; approving it signs nothing | a live mandate the owner approves with the passkey, compiled into the signer |
| Server locks | none | `SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION`, and the mainnet locks |

### The execution lock

`SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION` is a setting of the **server**, not of a key. Unless it is `on`, an order that adds live risk through this API is refused before anything is created:

```json
{
  "error": "execution_disabled",
  "message": "live execution through the trading API is disabled on this server (SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION is not \"on\"); nothing was created. Cancels and strictly reducing orders still pass."
}
```

Paper is never affected; cancels and strictly reducing orders pass even when the lock is closed. The production server (wallet.spboucher.ai) was reported running with the lock **on** when this page was written (2026-11-17) — the page cannot check it. Two ways to know: the owner's **Developer → Status** screen (service "execution"), or a `POST /orders/preview` of a live order, whose `refusals` would carry `{ "stage": "lock", "code": "execution_disabled" }`. `GET /risk` lists the other server locks under `capabilityLocks` (`deriv_live_mainnet`, `deriv_mandate_mainnet`, `deriv_gmx_live`, `broadcast`), each with `on` and `why`; the execution lock itself is not in that list.

### Before the first live order with a key

1. An authorized Hyperliquid venue account (app: Futures → Accounts, passkey).
2. A **live** trading mandate for the key, approved with the passkey, and bound to the key.
3. The risk limits of the live book set by the owner (Settings → Risk → Limits → Live): on a live book an absent limit **denies**, and a key never benefits from limits carried over from earlier versions.
4. The server locks open.

Preview first: `POST /orders/preview` runs every gate on the market as it is now and says what would refuse the order, without creating anything.

<a id="mandates"></a>

## Mandates

A **trading mandate** is the owner's written authorization for one principal (an API key or an agent): which accounts, which instruments, which order types, how much, how fast, until when. It is immutable — a change is a new mandate and the revocation of the old one — and it is checked on **every** order.

What a mandate fixes: mode (`paper` or `live`), accounts, instruments, order types, `maxCapitalUsd`, `maxOrderNotionalUsd`, `maxPositionNotionalUsd`, `maxLeverage`, `maxDailyLossUsd`, `floorEquityUsd`, order frequency (per minute, hour, day), `maxSlippageBps`, `maxFeeUsd`, a duration in days, and stop conditions.

- **Only the owner** creates, approves or revokes one (Settings → Risk → Trading mandates). The routes are outside this API: a key can do nothing to a mandate, its own included.
- A **paper** mandate signs nothing. A **live** mandate is compiled into the shape the isolated signer enforces and approved with the passkey: the signer then refuses, by itself, an order outside it.
- A revoked or expired mandate **winds down**: for 24 hours only cancels and strictly reducing orders pass (422 `mandate_wind_down`).
- A strictly reducing order is exempt from the limits that would stop an exit (capital, position, daily loss, frequency, stop conditions). It stays bound to the mandate's account, venue and instrument.
- A key or an agent closes only what its own fills opened (403 `position_not_attributable`).

An order outside the mandate is refused with **every** reason at once, and the refused order is recorded:

```json
{
  "error": "mandate_denied",
  "message": "the order is outside the mandate: ORDER_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED",
  "data": {
    "reasons": [
      "ORDER_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED"
    ],
    "order": {
      "id": "0xd51cebbed3c7d5e595f90d368ea832ad",
      "orderId": "0xd51cebbed3c7d5e595f90d368ea832ad",
      "mode": "paper",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "network": "mainnet",
      "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
      "clientId": "0xd51cebbed3c7d5e595f90d368ea832ad",
      "clientOrderId": "0xd51cebbed3c7d5e595f90d368ea832ad",
      "venueOrderId": null,
      "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "side": "buy",
      "type": "market",
      "size": "0.01396",
      "price": "64749",
      "triggerPrice": null,
      "reduceOnly": false,
      "tif": "ioc",
      "status": "REJECTED",
      "final": true,
      "reasonCode": "MANDATE_DENIED",
      "reason": "the order is outside the mandate: ORDER_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED",
      "rejectCode": "mandate_denied",
      "engineStatus": null,
      "filled": "0",
      "avgFillPrice": null,
      "acknowledged": false,
      "cancelRequested": false,
      "origin": "trading_api",
      "principal": {
        "kind": "api_key",
        "id": "spk_0123456789ab"
      },
      "mandateId": "84f954ea-0c25-4985-bc19-901d510964d7",
      "engine": "paper",
      "engineRef": null,
      "hash": "1aee5a4cdfc23da98dd02b104acef0677437ba9d866a24cf53c509e00b6d6a40",
      "notionalUsd": "903.89604",
      "projectionError": null,
      "version": 1,
      "createdAt": 1794953731495,
      "updatedAt": 1794953731502
    }
  }
}
```

### `data.reasons` of `mandate_denied`

| Reason | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `INVALID_INPUT` | the order or the usage figures could not be evaluated |
| `MANDATE_NOT_YET_VALID` | the mandate's validity window has not started |
| `MANDATE_ENDED` | the mandate has ended (past its end date and its wind-down) |
| `MANDATE_REVOKED` | the mandate was revoked |
| `WIND_DOWN_REDUCING_ONLY` | the mandate is winding down: only strictly reducing orders pass |
| `PRINCIPAL_MISMATCH` | the mandate was approved for another key or agent |
| `MANDATE_ID_MISMATCH` | the order names another mandate |
| `MODE_MISMATCH` | a paper mandate cannot place a live order, nor the reverse |
| `ACCOUNT_NOT_ALLOWED` | the account is not one of the mandate's |
| `VENUE_NOT_ALLOWED` | the venue is not one of the mandate's |
| `NETWORK_NOT_ALLOWED` | the network is not one of the mandate's |
| `INSTRUMENT_NOT_ALLOWED` | the instrument is not in the mandate's list |
| `MARKET_KIND_NOT_ALLOWED` | spot or perp is not allowed by the mandate |
| `ORDER_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED` | the order type (market, limit, stop, take-profit) is not in the mandate's list |
| `NOTIONAL_UNKNOWN` | the order cannot be valued (no reference price), so the limits cannot be checked |
| `ORDER_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED` | the order is above `maxOrderNotionalUsd` |
| `POSITION_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED` | the resulting position is above `maxPositionNotionalUsd` |
| `CAPITAL_EXCEEDED` | the capital in use after the order is above `maxCapitalUsd` |
| `LEVERAGE_EXCEEDED` | the leverage is above `maxLeverage` |
| `LEVERAGE_UNKNOWN` | the leverage the order would run under is not known |
| `DAILY_LOSS_REACHED` | today's realised loss under the mandate reached `maxDailyLossUsd` |
| `BELOW_FLOOR_EQUITY` | the equity of the mandated accounts is below `floorEquityUsd` |
| `RATE_PER_MINUTE` | the mandate's orders-per-minute limit is reached |
| `RATE_PER_HOUR` | the mandate's orders-per-hour limit is reached |
| `RATE_PER_DAY` | the mandate's orders-per-day limit is reached |
| `SLIPPAGE_ABOVE_MANDATE` | the order's slippage bound is above the mandate's `maxSlippageBps` |
| `FEE_ABOVE_MANDATE` | the order's fee cap is above the mandate's `maxFeeUsd` |
| `ORDER_OUTLIVES_MANDATE` | the order would stay valid after the mandate ends |
| `STOP_LOSS_GTE` | stop condition: the total loss under the mandate reached its threshold |
| `STOP_DRAWDOWN_GTE` | stop condition: the drawdown from the peak equity reached its threshold |
| `STOP_CONSECUTIVE_REJECTS_GTE` | stop condition: too many consecutive rejected orders |
| `STOP_DATA_STALE_GTE` | stop condition: the market data is too old |
| `STOP_SIGNER_UNREACHABLE_GTE` | stop condition: the signer has been unreachable for too long |

<a id="refusals"></a>

## Why an order is refused

An order goes through gates, in this order. The first that refuses answers; the error code tells which gate it was.

| # | Gate | Refuses with | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **The request** — schema, instrument, account | 400 `bad_request`, `invalid_order`; 404 `unknown_instrument`; 409 `ambiguous_symbol`, `account_required`; 422 `below_min_size`, `no_account` | the request |
| 2 | **The key** — scope, restrictions, a mandate bound to it | 403 `insufficient_scope`, `restricted`, `mandate_required` | another key, or the owner widens it |
| 3 | **The execution lock** (live only), then the key's own notional limits, then whose position it is | 403 `execution_disabled`; `key_limit_exceeded`; `position_not_attributable` | the operator; a smaller order |
| 4 | **The mandate** | 403 `mandate_denied` + `data.reasons`; `mandate_not_approved`, `mandate_integrity`; 422 `mandate_wind_down` | fit the mandate, or a new mandate |
| 5 | **The risk engine** — one engine for every principal, venue and mode | 422 `risk_denied` + `data.failed`, `data.checks`; 423 `kill_switch_active`, `read_only_mode` | read `data.checks` |
| 6 | **The venue engine's own rules** — tick, lot, price bands, collateral, the account policy; for live orders, the mainnet locks | 422 `price_not_on_tick`, `size_not_on_lot`, `price_deviation`, `insufficient_collateral`, …; 403 `mainnet_disabled` | the order; the operator |
| 7 | **The venue** | 422 `venue_rejected`; 503 `venue_unavailable` | read `message` |

Up to gate 3 nothing is created. From gate 4 on, the order **exists**: the refusal carries it in `data.order` with `status: "REJECTED"`, and it appears in `GET /orders`. A refusal is a 4xx; an outcome that is not known is **not** an error — the order is `UNKNOWN` and the server resolves it.

**Ask before you send.** `POST /orders/preview` runs the same gates and answers 200 with `wouldExecute` and `refusals: [{ stage, code, message }]` (`stage` is `key`, `lock`, `mandate`, `risk` or `engine`), every risk check with what was observed and its limit, and an estimate of fees and slippage.

### The risk engine

One engine judges every order — the owner's, an agent's, a key's; paper or live — before any signature. A refusal says which checks failed (`kill_switch` here), what was observed and what the limit was:

```json
{
  "error": "kill_switch_active",
  "message": "kill_switch: the kill switch is on: no new risk",
  "data": {
    "failed": [
      "kill_switch"
    ],
    "checks": [
      {
        "id": "kill_switch",
        "ok": false,
        "observed": "true",
        "limit": "false",
        "detail": "the kill switch is on: no new risk",
        "exempt": false
      }
    ],
    "decisionId": 21,
    "order": {
      "id": "0x6f3d33c8d9a43c3e0bf5a17784a5c1a7",
      "orderId": "0x6f3d33c8d9a43c3e0bf5a17784a5c1a7",
      "mode": "paper",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "network": "mainnet",
      "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
      "clientId": "0x6f3d33c8d9a43c3e0bf5a17784a5c1a7",
      "clientOrderId": "0x6f3d33c8d9a43c3e0bf5a17784a5c1a7",
      "venueOrderId": null,
      "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "side": "buy",
      "type": "market",
      "size": "0.00155",
      "price": "64754",
      "triggerPrice": null,
      "reduceOnly": false,
      "tif": "ioc",
      "status": "REJECTED",
      "final": true,
      "reasonCode": "KILL_SWITCH_ACTIVE",
      "reason": "kill_switch: the kill switch is on: no new risk",
      "rejectCode": "kill_switch_active",
      "engineStatus": null,
      "filled": "0",
      "avgFillPrice": null,
      "acknowledged": false,
      "cancelRequested": false,
      "origin": "trading_api",
      "principal": {
        "kind": "api_key",
        "id": "spk_0123456789ab"
      },
      "mandateId": "84f954ea-0c25-4985-bc19-901d510964d7",
      "engine": "paper",
      "engineRef": null,
      "hash": "ea251eb6fc7f290e903f8c8a23dc307c80181d4d35ee320e9d7aa65622218b3f",
      "notionalUsd": "100.3687",
      "projectionError": null,
      "version": 1,
      "createdAt": 1794953766532,
      "updatedAt": 1794953766906
    }
  }
}
```

`data.checks[].observed` and `.detail` are withheld (null / a neutral sentence) for the checks that reveal account figures when the key holds neither `wallet:read` nor `positions:read`.

| Check | Limit | Rule | Applies to a strictly reducing order |
|---|---|---|---|
| `input_valid` | — | the three inputs parse; the order's instrument is the context's; at most one position per venue and instrument; a spot sell does not exceed the holding supplied | yes |
| `read_only` | `context.readOnly` | read-only mode denies every order, reducing or not; only a cancel passes | yes |
| `kill_switch` | `context.killSwitch` | the kill switch denies every order that is not strictly reducing | no (exempt) |
| `reduce_only_valid` | — | an order flagged reduce-only must be opposite to an existing position and no larger than it (it can neither increase nor flip) | yes |
| `venue_allowed` | `allowedVenues` | the venue is in the allow-list | no (exempt) |
| `instrument_allowed` | `allowedInstruments` | the instrument is in the allow-list | no (exempt) |
| `instrument_status` | — | the instrument is active on the venue (a halted or reduce-only instrument takes no new risk) | no (exempt) |
| `signer_status` | — | live orders: the signer is reachable and not halted | no (exempt) |
| `venue_connection` | — | live orders: the venue connection is up | no (exempt) |
| `price_freshness` | `maxPriceAgeMs, minPriceSources` | at least minPriceSources sources of the instrument are no older (and no further in the future) than their maximum age | only without a limit price |
| `price_consistency` | `maxSourceDeviationBps` | (highest − lowest) / lowest over the fresh sources is within the limit; inconsistent data denies | only without a limit price |
| `order_notional` | `maxOrderNotional` | notional of the order (its own notional, or size × the highest of reference, limit and trigger price) | no (exempt) |
| `daily_notional` | `maxDailyNotional` | today's accepted notional plus this order | no (exempt) |
| `order_rate_minute` | `maxOrdersPerMinute` | orders already accepted in the last minute are below the limit | no (exempt) |
| `order_rate_hour` | `maxOrdersPerHour` | orders already accepted in the last hour are below the limit | no (exempt) |
| `order_rate_day` | `maxOrdersPerDay` | orders already accepted today are below the limit | no (exempt) |
| `position_size` | `maxPositionNotional` | resulting position on the instrument, at the reference price | no (exempt) |
| `order_leverage` | `maxOrderLeverage` | perp: the leverage setting the order runs under is known, within the limit and within the venue's maximum | no (exempt) |
| `account_leverage` | `maxAccountLeverage` | gross exposure after the order / total equity (margin.effectiveLeverage); equity ≤ 0 denies | no (exempt) |
| `gross_exposure` | `maxGrossExposure` | Σ \|size\| · mark over all venues (margin.grossExposure) | no (exempt) |
| `net_exposure` | `maxNetExposure` | \|Σ long − Σ short\| over all venues (margin.netExposure) | no (exempt) |
| `asset_exposure` | `maxAssetExposure` | \|net\| exposure of the order's asset over all venues (margin.exposureByAsset) | no (exempt) |
| `venue_exposure` | `maxVenueExposure` | gross exposure on the order's venue | no (exempt) |
| `collateral_concentration` | `maxVenueCollateralShareBps` | share of the total equity held on the order's venue | no (exempt) |
| `daily_loss` | `maxDailyLoss` | today's loss has not reached the limit | no (exempt) |
| `total_loss` | `maxTotalLoss` | the loss since inception has not reached the limit | no (exempt) |
| `drawdown` | `maxDrawdownBps` | (peak − current) / peak has not reached the limit | no (exempt) |
| `liquidation_distance` | `minLiquidationDistanceBps` | perp: estimated liquidation price after the order (margin.liquidationPriceCross / liquidationPriceAtLeverage) is at least the limit away from the reference price | no (exempt) |
| `slippage` | `maxSlippageBps (and the order's own maxSlippageBps when lower)` | average price of walking the book (router.walkBook) vs the mid; a book that cannot fill a market order denies | no (exempt) |
| `min_liquidity` | `minLiquidity` | depth on the taking side within withinBps of the mid ≥ multiple × order size | no (exempt) |
| `context_complete` | — | every account of the book could be read; when one cannot, exposure is not known and new risk is denied | no (exempt) |

Three things worth knowing:

- On a **live** book, a limit that was never set **denies** (the detail says "no limit is configured"). The owner sets them in Settings → Risk.
- With stale prices, the way out of a position is a reduce-only **limit** order: it names its own price and is exempt from the price checks.
- The **kill switch** blocks every new risk for everybody; cancels and strictly reducing orders pass. A key with `positions:manage` can engage it (`POST /risk/kill-switch`) and **cannot** release it. **Read-only mode** lets only cancels pass. Neither is the signer's emergency halt (423 `halted`), which is lifted on the signer machine.

### The venue engine

The last local gate is the engine that would execute the order, with the account's own policy. Its codes are the ones a trading program meets most — a limit price too far from the mark, for instance:

```json
{
  "error": "price_deviation",
  "message": "price 51000 is more than 200 bps away from the mark 64433",
  "data": {
    "order": "{ … the order, status REJECTED … }"
  }
}
```

An operation whose engine can add a code of its own is marked **open** in its error list (`x-spw-errors-open` in the OpenAPI document): treat an unknown 4xx code by its HTTP status.

<a id="order-states"></a>

## Order states

An order's `status` is one of twelve states; `final` is true for four of them.

```text
CREATED → VALIDATED → AWAITING_APPROVAL → APPROVED → SUBMITTING → SUBMITTED → PARTIALLY_FILLED → FILLED
                                                          └→ UNKNOWN → (the server asks the venue) → any of the above, or CANCELLED / REJECTED / FAILED
final: FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, FAILED
```

| `status` | Final | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `CREATED` | — | recorded; nothing checked yet |
| `VALIDATED` | — | passed the mandate and the risk engine |
| `AWAITING_APPROVAL` | — | an owner order waits for the passkey in the app (never the case for a key) |
| `APPROVED` | — | approved; about to be handed to its engine |
| `SUBMITTING` | — | being sent to the venue (or the paper engine) |
| `SUBMITTED` | — | accepted by the venue and resting; nothing filled yet |
| `PARTIALLY_FILLED` | — | partly filled; the rest is still working |
| `FILLED` | yes | completely filled. Final |
| `CANCELLED` | yes | withdrawn, expired or cancelled. Final |
| `REJECTED` | yes | refused by a rule: validation, mandate, risk, signer policy or the venue. Final; `reasonCode` and `rejectCode` say which |
| `FAILED` | yes | a technical failure after which it is PROVEN that no order exists at the venue. Final |
| `UNKNOWN` | — | the outcome at the venue is not known yet. NOT final and NOT a failure: the server resolves it by asking the venue — keep reading the order, do not send it again under a new key |

- Decide on `status` and `final`. `engineStatus` is the executing engine's own word for the same moment, for information.
- **`UNKNOWN` is not a failure.** A network error after the request may have left makes the outcome unknown; the server reconciles with the venue. Wait — do not place the order again under another key.
- Nothing becomes `FILLED` without an event that comes from the venue (or the paper engine).
- `reasonCode` is the closed reason of the current state; `rejectCode` is the API error code of the refusal; `reason` is a sentence for people.
- Order types: `market`, `limit`, `stop_market`, `stop_limit`, `take_profit_market`, `take_profit_limit`. Time in force: `gtc`, `ioc`, `alo` (add-liquidity-only). A market order is bounded by `slippageBps` (1 to 500), not by a price.

<a id="idempotency"></a>

## Idempotency

Every mutating call needs an `Idempotency-Key` header: 8 to 100 characters among letters, digits, `.` `_` `:` `-`. It makes a retry safe.

| You send | The server |
|---|---|
| a new key | runs the operation, stores the answer (a 200 **or** a 4xx refusal) with a hash of the request (method, path, canonical body) |
| the same key, the same request | returns the stored answer with `Idempotent-Replay: true`; **nothing runs again** |
| the same key, a different request | 409 `idempotency_conflict` |
| the same key while the first call is still running | 409 `idempotency_in_progress` — wait and resend |
| no key | 400 `idempotency` |

```json
{
  "error": "idempotency_conflict",
  "message": "this Idempotency-Key was used with a different request"
}
```

- The namespace is the **principal**: two keys can use the same string.
- An answer ≥ 500 is **not** stored: nothing is assumed to have happened, the same key can be sent again — and should be. After a timeout or a 5xx, resend with the **same** key; never mint a new one for a retry.
- Stored answers are kept 24 hours.
- **An order has a second, permanent identity**: `clientOrderId` in the body (default: the Idempotency-Key). The order id is derived from it and from the principal, so the same `clientOrderId` is the same order for ever — `idempotent: true` in the answer says "this already existed, nothing was sent again".
- `X-SPW-Timestamp` (optional, Unix seconds): when present, a call more than 300 s from the server clock is refused (400 `stale_request`) — a guard against a delayed replay by a proxy.

Operations that take **no** Idempotency-Key, because they change nothing or are naturally idempotent: `POST /orders/preview` and `POST /risk/kill-switch`.

A good key is the name of the intent, not a random value made at send time: `rebalance-2026-11-17-btc`. Persist it before sending if the process can crash.

<a id="pagination"></a>

## Pagination

Three conventions, by list:

| Lists | How | End |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /orders`, `GET /activity`, `GET /nfts`, `GET /nfts/activity` | `limit` + opaque `cursor`: pass `nextCursor` of a page as `cursor` of the next, **with the same filters** | `nextCursor: null` |
| `GET /agents/{id}/signals`, `/journal`, `/orders` | `limit` + `before` (Unix ms: rows strictly before it) — pass the `ts` of the last row | fewer rows than `limit` |
| `GET /markets` and the other lists | not paginated; `limit` cuts, and `total` + `truncated` say so | — |

Cursor pages are newest first, in one stable order (time, then id): a row created after the first page was read is not inserted into the pages that follow. A cursor that did not come from this list answers 400 `invalid_cursor`. `GET /nfts/market/events` has one cursor **per source**: send it with `provider`.

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "kind": "fill",
      "mode": "paper",
      "at": "2026-11-17T22:15:31.480Z",
      "id": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:paper-f-6",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
      "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "side": "sell",
      "price": "64426",
      "size": "0.00078",
      "fee": "0.022613526",
      "closedPnl": "-0.00468"
    },
    {
      "kind": "fill",
      "mode": "paper",
      "at": "2026-11-17T22:15:31.456Z",
      "id": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:paper-f-4",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
      "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "side": "sell",
      "price": "64426",
      "size": "0.00077",
      "fee": "0.022323609",
      "closedPnl": "-0.00462"
    }
  ],
  "nextCursor": "WyIxNzk0OTUzNzMxNDU2MDAwIiwiZiIsIjZhMmM4MzUxLWNkNzktNDRlMi1iNzVhLWYzOTI4MGQwZWJjMjpwYXBlci1mLTQiXQ"
}
```

Both SDKs follow the cursor for you: `iterateOrders` / `iterateActivity` (TypeScript), `iter_orders` / `iter_activity` (Python).

<a id="rate-limits"></a>

## Rate limits

Per key, in fixed one-minute windows: 120 calls a minute by default, set per key up to 600. Every answer to a key carries:

| Header | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `RateLimit-Limit` | the key's calls per minute |
| `RateLimit-Remaining` | calls left in the current window |
| `RateLimit-Reset` | seconds until the window resets |
| `Retry-After` | on a 429 only: seconds to wait |

```http
HTTP/1.1 429 Too Many Requests
RateLimit-Limit: 3
RateLimit-Remaining: 0
RateLimit-Reset: 60
Retry-After: 60

{"error":"rate_limited","message":"too many requests for this key"}
```

- Every call counts, a refused one and a 404 included.
- The window is in the memory of the API process: a restart resets it.
- Failed authentications are counted per source address, apart: 10 a minute, then 429 `auth_throttled` with `Retry-After: 60`.
- The owner's session is limited per address instead (240 a minute) and carries no `RateLimit-*` header.
- Venues have their own limits: a venue that throttles the wallet answers 503 `venue_rate_limited`, which is not about your key.

Both SDKs wait and retry on 429 (reads, and writes because they carry an Idempotency-Key). For live prices do not poll: use the [stream](#stream).

<a id="errors"></a>

## Errors

Every error has the same body, whatever the status:

```json
{
  "error": "<stable machine code>",
  "message": "<sentence for people, English>",
  "data": {
    "…": "machine-readable detail, on refusals that have some"
  }
}
```

Branch on `error`, never on `message`. `data` may carry: `order` (the order a refusal is about), `reasons` (mandate), `failed` and `checks` (risk), `questions` (an ambiguous command), `code` and `token` (a parse error). `ambiguous_symbol` carries `candidates` at the top level.

| Status | Class | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | the request is not valid | no — fix it |
| 401 | no usable credential | no |
| 403 | authenticated, and refused: scope, restriction, mandate, server lock | no |
| 404 | the object does not exist; or the path is not an operation (`not_found`) | no |
| 409 | a conflict with the state of the object, or a choice is needed | after resolving it |
| 422 | a valid request that cannot be carried out now | after changing the order |
| 423 | blocked by the kill switch, read-only mode or a halt | when the owner releases it |
| 429 | too many requests | yes, after `Retry-After` |
| 500 | server error; a mutating call may or may not have run | yes, with the **same** Idempotency-Key |
| 501 | declared but not connected; nothing was done | no |
| 502, 503 | a backing service (venue, signer, provider) failed; nothing was done | yes, with backoff |

A path that is not an operation, or a known path with another method, answers in the same model:

```json
{
  "error": "not_found",
  "message": "no such operation in the trading API: see GET /api/v1/trading/openapi.json"
}
```

A body that does not match the schema says only that — the server does not echo which field (check the request against the operation's schema):

```json
{
  "error": "bad_request",
  "message": "invalid request"
}
```

Both SDKs turn these into typed exceptions (`AuthenticationError`, `ScopeDenied`, `MandateRequired`, `MandateDenied`, `RiskDenied`, `ExecutionDisabled`, `AmbiguousCommand`, `RateLimited`, `ValidationError`, …) with `code`, `status`, `requestId` and `order`. A mutating call that got no trustworthy answer raises `OutcomeUnknown` — not a failure — with the Idempotency-Key to resume with.

The full list, code by code, is the [error catalogue](#error-catalogue) at the end; each operation lists the codes it can answer.

<a id="commands"></a>

## Commands

`POST /commands` takes one line of a small deterministic grammar — no model, no guess — in English or French. It is the same machinery as the order operations, behind a sentence.

```text
LONG|SHORT <asset>-PERP <notional> [x<lev>] [LIMIT <px>] [SL <px>] [TP <px>] [ON <venue>]
CLOSE <asset>-PERP [pct%] [ON <venue>]
BUY <asset> <amount> <quote> [ON <venue>]          (spot — the owner only)
SELL <asset> <amount|pct%> [FOR <quote>] [ON <venue>]   (spot — the owner only)
CANCEL ORDER <id>          CANCEL ALL [ON <venue>]
SHOW POSITIONS | BALANCES | ORDERS | RISK
STOP ALL AGENTS            KILL
```

French spellings are accepted for the keywords (`ACHETER`, `VENDRE`, `FERMER`, `ANNULER`, `AFFICHER`, `SUR`, `URGENCE`, …); numbers are plain decimals.

- The **scope** checked is the one of the operation the line resolves to: an order needs `orders:execute` (`orders:prepare` with `dryRun`), CLOSE `positions:manage`, CANCEL `orders:cancel`, SHOW the matching read scope, KILL `positions:manage`.
- `dryRun: true` answers what the line resolves to and does nothing.
- **Nothing is assumed.** Venue, network, leverage, margin mode, fee cap, slippage bound and order validity come from the line or from the owner's stored defaults (he saves them from the command line of the app's Trade screen; owner routes `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/developer/command-defaults`). What neither says is asked back, and nothing executes:

```json
{
  "error": "ambiguous_command",
  "message": "the command and the owner's defaults do not settle everything: answer the questions (or set the defaults) and send it again. Nothing was executed.",
  "data": {
    "questions": [
      {
        "field": "network",
        "options": [
          "mainnet"
        ],
        "message": "on which network? The defaults name none for BTC-PERP"
      },
      {
        "field": "orderTtlMs",
        "options": [],
        "message": "no order validity is pre-approved: how long may the order wait before it is sent?"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The answer has a `kind`: `query` (the read answer is in `data`), `control` (a cancel, the kill switch) or `order` — with `executed: false` for a dry run (`order` is then the canonical order) and `executed: true` otherwise (`order` is the placed order, `canonical` its canonical form). Recorded, `LONG BTC-PERP 100` executed on paper answered `kind: "order"`, `command: "perp_open"`, `order.status: "FILLED"`.

A line that is not a sentence of the grammar:

```json
{
  "error": "parse_error",
  "message": "unknown command",
  "data": {
    "code": "unknown_command",
    "token": 0
  }
}
```

<a id="webhooks"></a>

## Webhooks

Webhooks push events to an HTTPS endpoint of yours. The owner manages them in **Settings → Developer / API → Webhooks** (at most 10 endpoints); the signing secret (`whsec_…`) is shown once.

| Event | Payload (`data`) | When |
|---|---|---|
| `key.revoked` | `{ keyId, name, reason }` | an API key was revoked (or replaced by a rotation) |
| `risk.kill_switch` | `{ source: "signer", halted: true } \| { source: "risk_engine", engaged, by, reason }` | the emergency halt of the signer was triggered (source "signer"), or the global kill switch of the risk engine was engaged or released (source "risk_engine") |
| `agent.updated` | `{ agentId, kind, state, mode }` | an agent was started, paused or stopped (by the owner, an API key or the risk-management agent) |
| `risk.proposal` | `{ supervisorId, agentId, mode, action, code, acted, message, metrics }` | the risk-management agent proposes a reduction or a pause, or acted (paused an agent, engaged the kill switch) |
| `order.updated` | `{ orderId, mode, venue, instrument, status, previous, event, reason, seq, principal, mandateId }` | an order changed state: one event per transition of the unified order state machine, for every principal, mode and venue |

Payloads carry identifiers and states only — no secret, no key material, no amount. On `order.updated`, read the order with `GET /orders/{id}`.

### What you receive

```http
POST /your/endpoint HTTP/1.1
Content-Type: application/json
X-SPW-Timestamp: 1794953687
X-SPW-Signature: v1=df38f5cf8ef5b56e0c43c3b4fca7755bfcde2892199e8bfe8dc974fee3863b7b
X-SPW-Event: key.revoked
X-SPW-Delivery: 42

{"app":"sp-wallet","id":"ed1fb6d3-1b5c-4bf6-b8cd-2237d8b2b38c","event":"key.revoked","ts":"2026-11-17T22:14:47.655Z","data":{"keyId":"spk_0123456789ab","name":"old bot","reason":"rotated to spk_ba9876543210"}}
```

### Verifying the signature

`X-SPW-Signature` is `v1=` + the hex HMAC-SHA-256 of `"<X-SPW-Timestamp>.<raw body>"` with the endpoint's secret. Verify over the **raw bytes** received (not a re-serialised object), compare in constant time, and refuse a timestamp more than 300 s from your clock.

The example above is real arithmetic: with the secret `whsec_EXAMPLE_ONLY_0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuv`, the string signed is `1794953687.` followed by the body, and the HMAC is the value shown. Check your implementation against it.

```ts
import { constructWebhookEvent, WebhookVerificationError } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

// rawBody: the bytes exactly as received (Buffer / Uint8Array / string)
try {
  const event = await constructWebhookEvent({
    secret: process.env.SP_WALLET_WEBHOOK_SECRET!,
    timestamp: req.headers['x-spw-timestamp'],
    signature: req.headers['x-spw-signature'],
    body: rawBody,
  });
  if (!seen.has(event.id)) { seen.add(event.id); handle(event.event, event.data); }
  res.writeHead(200).end('ok');
} catch (e) {
  res.writeHead(e instanceof WebhookVerificationError ? 400 : 500).end();
}
```

```python
from sp_wallet import WebhookVerificationError, construct_webhook_event

# raw: the bytes exactly as received
try:
    event = construct_webhook_event(secret=SECRET, timestamp=headers.get("x-spw-timestamp"),
                                    signature=headers.get("x-spw-signature"), body=raw)
except WebhookVerificationError:
    return 400
if event["id"] not in seen:
    seen.add(event["id"])
    handle(event["event"], event["data"])
return 200
```

```python
# without the SDK
import hashlib, hmac, time

def verify(secret: str, timestamp: str, signature: str, raw: bytes) -> bool:
    if abs(time.time() - int(timestamp)) > 300:
        return False
    want = "v1=" + hmac.new(secret.encode(), timestamp.encode() + b"." + raw, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
    return hmac.compare_digest(want, signature)
```

### Delivery

- **At least once.** Deduplicate on the envelope's `id` (the same for every attempt of one event; `X-SPW-Delivery` is the delivery row).
- A delivery succeeds on any 2xx. Otherwise it is retried after 30 s, 2 min, 10 min, 1 h, 6 h; after 6 attempts it is `failed`. The Webhooks screen shows every delivery with its last status.
- HTTPS only, no credentials in the URL, no redirect followed, 8 seconds to answer.
- Order is not guaranteed: use `data.seq` of `order.updated` (it grows with each transition of one order).

<a id="stream"></a>

## Streaming (websocket)

Live market data — quotes, order books, trades, candles, venue status — is a websocket, which OpenAPI cannot describe. It is read-only: nothing is placed or cancelled over it.

```text
wss://wallet.spboucher.ai/api/v1/trading/stream
```

- **Scope**: `markets:read`. The key goes in the `Authorization` header of the handshake — never in the URL.
- A client that cannot set a header (a browser) asks for a single-use ticket: `POST /api/v1/trading/stream/ticket` (same key, same scope) answers `{ ticket, expiresAt, ttlMs, url, protocol }`; connect to `…/stream?ticket=…` within 30 s.
- A key's venue and instrument restrictions apply per subscription (`error restricted`).
- At most 4 connections per key and 300 subscriptions per connection.
- A stream connection is not in the key's usage log; the ticket request is.

```ts
import { SPWalletStream } from '@sp-wallet/trading-sdk';

const stream = new SPWalletStream();   // SP_WALLET_API_KEY, SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
await stream.subscribe([{ type: 'quote', id: 'hyperliquid:BTC' }, { type: 'status', id: 'hyperliquid' }]);
for await (const m of stream) {
  if (m.type === 'quote') console.log(m.id, m.bid, m.ask, m.mark, m.stale ? '(stale)' : '');
}
```

```python
# pip install "sp-wallet-python[stream]"
import asyncio
from sp_wallet import SPWalletStream
from sp_wallet.stream import Quote

async def main() -> None:
    stream = SPWalletStream()   # SP_WALLET_API_KEY, SP_WALLET_BASE_URL
    await stream.subscribe([{"type": "quote", "id": "hyperliquid:BTC"}, {"type": "status", "id": "hyperliquid"}])
    async for m in stream:
        if isinstance(m, Quote):
            print(m.id, m.bid, m.ask, m.mark, "(stale)" if m.stale else "")

asyncio.run(main())
```

### Protocol (version 1), in short

JSON text frames; every server frame has `"v": 1` and a `type`. Prices, sizes and rates are decimal strings; times are milliseconds.

```json
// client → server
{ "op": "subscribe", "reqId": 7, "channels": [ { "type": "quote",  "id": "hyperliquid:BTC" },
                                               { "type": "book",   "id": "hyperliquid:BTC", "depth": 10 },
                                               { "type": "trades", "id": "hyperliquid:BTC" },
                                               { "type": "candle", "id": "hyperliquid:BTC", "interval": "1m" },
                                               { "type": "status", "id": "*" } ] }
{ "op": "unsubscribe", "channels": [ { "type": "quote", "id": "hyperliquid:BTC" } ] }
{ "op": "ping", "t": 42 }
```

| Server frame | Carries |
|---|---|
| `hello` | first frame: `connId`, `heartbeatMs`, the limits |
| `subscribed` | one per channel, with the last known value at once (`snapshot`, `snapshotAgeMs`) and `staleAfterMs` |
| `quote` | bid, ask, mid, mark, index, funding, open interest, `seq`, `stale` — a full state each time |
| `book` | a `snapshot`, then `delta`s (`prevSeq` must equal the last `seq` applied; size `"0"` removes a price; `checksum` is a CRC-32 of the top ten levels) |
| `trade` | a batch, oldest first; the first after a subscription is recent history (`snapshot: true`) |
| `candle` | the bar in progress or a new bar |
| `status` | per venue: `connected`, `reconnecting`, `stale`, `down`, `idle` |
| `heartbeat`, `pong`, `dropped`, `unsubscribed`, `error` | housekeeping; an `error` never closes the connection |

Close code **4001** means the key is no longer active: do not reconnect with it. `1001` (server restart) — reconnect. The full protocol, the limits and the staleness rules are in `docs/V11-QUOTE-ENGINE.md`; both SDKs implement reconnection, resubscription and the order-book checks (`OrderBook`).

Order and position updates are **not** on the stream: use the `order.updated` webhook, or poll `GET /orders/{id}` (`waitForOrder` / `wait_for_order`).

<a id="operations"></a>

## Operations

| Method | Path | Operation | Permission | Idempotency-Key |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/openapi.json` | [`getOpenApi`](#op-getOpenApi) — This document | any valid key | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/markets` | [`listMarkets`](#op-listMarkets) — Instruments across venues (perpetuals) and the spot market-data symbols | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/markets/compare` | [`compareMarkets`](#op-compareMarkets) — Compare the estimated cost of one position across venues | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}` | [`getMarket`](#op-getMarket) — One market: instrument, ticker and data quality | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}/orderbook` | [`getOrderbook`](#op-getOrderbook) — Order book of one market | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}/candles` | [`getCandles`](#op-getCandles) — Closed bars of one market | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/private-markets` | [`listPrivateMarkets`](#op-listPrivateMarkets) — Synthetic contracts on private companies, venue by venue, with sourced valuations | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/private-markets/companies/{slug}` | [`getPrivateMarketCompany`](#op-getPrivateMarketCompany) — Every contract on one private company, side by side (never merged) | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/private-markets/{id}` | [`getPrivateMarket`](#op-getPrivateMarket) — One private-company contract: definition, valuation breakdown, candles, top of book | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/portfolio` | [`getPortfolio`](#op-getPortfolio) — Wallet value and derivative accounts, per mode | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/balances` | [`getBalances`](#op-getBalances) — On-chain balances of the wallet | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/activity` | [`getActivity`](#op-getActivity) — Wallet activity and fills, newest first | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/nfts` | [`listNfts`](#op-listNfts) — NFTs of the wallet (what the chain confirmed as held) | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/nfts/item` | [`getNft`](#op-getNft) — One NFT of the wallet: identity, ownership, creator metadata, sources | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/nfts/activity` | [`getNftActivity`](#op-getNftActivity) — On-chain events of one NFT of the wallet | `wallet:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/nfts/market` | [`getNftMarket`](#op-getNftMarket) — Market data of any NFT, one row per source | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/nfts/market/events` | [`getNftMarketEvents`](#op-getNftMarketEvents) — Market events of any NFT (sales, listings, offers), per source | `markets:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/positions` | [`listPositions`](#op-listPositions) — Open positions, paper and live separated by `mode` | `positions:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/orders` | [`listOrders`](#op-listOrders) — Orders, newest first | `orders:read` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/orders` | [`placeOrder`](#op-placeOrder) — Place an order | `orders:execute` + mandate | required |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/orders/{id}` | [`getOrder`](#op-getOrder) — One order and its fills | `orders:read` | — |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/trading/orders/{id}` | [`cancelOrder`](#op-cancelOrder) — Cancel an order | `orders:cancel` | required |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/strategies` | [`listStrategies`](#op-listStrategies) — Strategy library and the versions pinned to agents | `strategies:read` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/strategies` | [`createStrategy`](#op-createStrategy) — Register a library strategy version on an agent | `strategies:manage` | required |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents` | [`listAgents`](#op-listAgents) — Trading agents | `agents:read` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/agents` | [`createAgent`](#op-createAgent) — Create an agent (from a template, or a perp agent) | `agents:manage` | required |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/risk` | [`getRisk`](#op-getRisk) — Risk state: kill switch, signer limits, capability locks, account policies | `positions:read` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/orders/preview` | [`previewOrder`](#op-previewOrder) — Preview an order: every check it would meet, and an estimate | `orders:prepare` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/positions/{id}/close` | [`closePosition`](#op-closePosition) — Close a position | `positions:manage` + mandate | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/positions/{id}/reduce` | [`reducePosition`](#op-reducePosition) — Reduce a position | `positions:manage` + mandate | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/commands` | [`runCommand`](#op-runCommand) — Run one line of the trading command grammar | `orders:prepare` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/risk/kill-switch` | [`engageKillSwitch`](#op-engageKillSwitch) — Engage the global kill switch | `positions:manage` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/backtest` | [`backtestStrategy`](#op-backtestStrategy) — Run the backtest of a strategy version (asynchronous job) | `strategies:manage` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/start` | [`startStrategy`](#op-startStrategy) — Start the agent a strategy version is pinned to | `strategies:manage` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/stop` | [`stopStrategy`](#op-stopStrategy) — Stop the agent a strategy version is pinned to | `strategies:manage` | required |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/templates` | [`listAgentTemplates`](#op-listAgentTemplates) — The seven built-in agents: family, engine, modes, installed strategies | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}` | [`getAgent`](#op-getAgent) — One agent: model, strategy, limits, mandate reference, backtest reports (labelled), worker state | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/signals` | [`listAgentSignals`](#op-listAgentSignals) — Signal history of one agent, ONE mode | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/journal` | [`listAgentJournal`](#op-listAgentJournal) — Activity journal of one agent, ONE mode | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/orders` | [`listAgentOrders`](#op-listAgentOrders) — Order history of one agent, ONE mode, in the unified order states | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/performance` | [`getAgentPerformance`](#op-getAgentPerformance) — Computed performance of one agent, ONE mode | `agents:read` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/trading/jobs/{id}` | [`getJob`](#op-getJob) — One agent job (backtest, wind-down): status and result | `agents:read` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/start` | [`startAgent`](#op-startAgent) — Start an agent in research, paper or live mode | `agents:manage` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/pause` | [`pauseAgent`](#op-pauseAgent) — Pause an agent | `agents:manage` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/stop` | [`stopAgent`](#op-stopAgent) — Stop an agent (always allowed) | `agents:manage` | required |
| POST | `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/backtest` | [`backtestAgent`](#op-backtestAgent) — Run a backtest for an agent (asynchronous job) | `agents:manage` | required |

<a id="tag-meta"></a>

### Meta

The contract itself.

<a id="op-getOpenApi"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/openapi.json`

**This document.** Operation id `getOpenApi`.

- **Permission:** any valid key
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

OpenAPI 3.1 document generated from the route schemas. Any valid key or the owner session.

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/openapi.json" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const doc = await client.openapi();
```

```python
doc = client.openapi()
```

**Example response** — 200, written from the schema (not recorded). Cut: the real document describes every operation, schema and error code.

```json
{
  "openapi": "3.1.0",
  "info": {
    "title": "SP Trading API",
    "version": "1.0.0"
  },
  "paths": {
    "/api/v1/trading/markets": {
      "get": {
        "operationId": "listMarkets"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `openapi` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |

<a id="tag-markets"></a>

### Markets

Public market data: instruments across venues, tickers, order books, candles, the venue comparator, and the synthetic contracts on private companies. Scope `markets:read`. Reading a venue does not mean it can be traded: live orders with a key exist on Hyperliquid only.

<a id="op-listMarkets"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/markets`

**Instruments across venues (perpetuals) and the spot market-data symbols.** Operation id `listMarkets`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Each row carries the time its source produced it (`ts`) and a `stale` flag. A venue that cannot be read is listed in `errors`; when no source answers the call fails with 503. An unknown `venue` answers 404 `unknown_venue`. Not paginated: `total` and `truncated` tell when `limit` cut the list.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `venue` | query | `string` | — | venue id: hyperliquid, gmx, dydx, spot — 0–40 characters |
| `kind` | query | `"perpetual" \| "spot"` | — | perpetual = a venue instrument; spot = a market-data symbol |
| `q` | query | `string` | — | case-insensitive match on id, symbol or base — 0–40 characters |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…5000` | — | rows returned at most; `total` and `truncated` say when it cut the list — default 2000 |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets?venue=hyperliquid&q=BTC&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { markets } = await client.listMarkets({ venue: 'hyperliquid', q: 'BTC', limit: 3 });
```

```python
markets = client.list_markets(venue="hyperliquid", q="BTC", limit=3).markets
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json

    {
```

| `markets` | `array of object` | yes |  |

| `markets[].stale` | `boolean` | yes | true when the row is older than its freshness bound or the source could not be refreshed |
| `total` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | rows matching the filters, before `limit` |

<a id="op-compareMarkets"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/markets/compare`

**Compare the estimated cost of one position across venues.** Operation id `compareMarkets`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

One row per venue for a common underlying, from the real instrument, order book (GMX: pool figures), ticker, fee schedule and funding rate read now: spread, depth within 10/25/50 bps, slippage for the notional, fees, funding expected over the horizon (current rate held constant), collateral, liquidation estimate and the itemised total. Every figure is an estimate; a missing input gives null with its reason. Venues the wallet cannot trade on are shown and flagged. `discrepancies` are estimates with their assumptions and risks, never a recommendation.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `underlying` | query | `string` | — | common underlying, e.g. BTC (see GET /markets-hub/underlyings) — default "BTC" · pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,15}$ |
| `side` | query | `"long" \| "short"` | — | direction of the position compared — default "long" |
| `notional` | query | `string` | — | position notional in USD (not the collateral) — default "1000" · pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,11})(\.\d{1,8})?$ |
| `leverage` | query | `string` | — | leverage of the position: collateral = notional / leverage — default "1" · pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,11})(\.\d{1,8})?$ |
| `horizonHours` | query | `string` | — | expected holding period; funding is the current rate held constant over it — default "24" · pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,3})(\.\d{1,4})?$ |
| `liquidity` | query | `"taker" \| "maker"` | — | how the entry and the exit are executed, for the fee — default "taker" |
| `minNetEdgeBps` | query | `string` | — | a discrepancy estimate above this is flagged — default "0" · pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,4})(\.\d{1,4})?$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets/compare?underlying=BTC&side=long&notional=1000&leverage=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const cmp = await client.compareMarkets({ underlying: 'BTC', side: 'long', notional: '1000', leverage: '2' });
```

```python
cmp = client.compare_markets(underlying="BTC", side="long", notional="1000", leverage="2")
```

<a id="op-getMarket"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}`

**One market: instrument, ticker and data quality.** Operation id `getMarket`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

`instrument` carries what an order must respect: `lotSize`, `tickSize`, `minOrderSize`, `minNotional`, `maxLeverage`. A bare symbol that exists on several venues answers 409 `ambiguous_symbol` with the candidates. 404: `unknown_venue` (the part before the colon), `unknown_instrument` (the venue does not list it), `unknown_market` (a spot symbol without data, or a bare symbol nothing answers to).

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `symbol` | path | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`, `spot:ETH`); a bare symbol is accepted when it is not ambiguous — 1–120 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets/hyperliquid:BTC" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { instrument, ticker } = await client.getMarket('hyperliquid:BTC');
```

```python
market = client.get_market("hyperliquid:BTC")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

| `instrument.maxLeverage` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `instrument.marginModes` | `array of "cross" \| "isolated"` | yes |  |
| `instrument.fundingIntervalSec` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes |  |
| `instrument.markPriceMethod` | `string` | yes |  |
| `instrument.status` | `"active" \| "reduce_only" \| "halted"` | yes |  |
| `instrument.category` | `"crypto" \| "equity" \| "pre_ipo" \| "commodity" \| "fx"` | — | what the underlying is, when the venue's own metadata says it |
| `ticker` | `object \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.instrument` | `string` | yes |  |
| `ticker.mark` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `ticker.index` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.mid` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.funding` | `string \| null` | yes | per funding interval |
| `ticker.openInterest` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.volume24h` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.change24h` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `ticker.ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | milliseconds since the Unix epoch |
| `quality` | `object \| null` | yes | spot only: data quality per interval |
| `ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | milliseconds since the Unix epoch |
| `source` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`unknown_instrument`](#err-unknown_instrument), [`unknown_market`](#err-unknown_market), [`unknown_venue`](#err-unknown_venue) |
| 409 | [`ambiguous_symbol`](#err-ambiguous_symbol) |
| 422 | [`venue_rejected`](#err-venue_rejected) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |
| 503 | [`venue_rate_limited`](#err-venue_rate_limited), [`venue_timeout`](#err-venue_timeout), [`venue_unavailable`](#err-venue_unavailable) |

<a id="op-getOrderbook"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}/orderbook`

**Order book of one market.** Operation id `getOrderbook`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Venues without an order book (GMX, spot data) answer 422 `no_orderbook`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `symbol` | path | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`, `spot:ETH`); a bare symbol is accepted when it is not ambiguous — 1–120 characters |
| `depth` | query | `integer 1…50` | — | price levels per side — default 20 |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets/hyperliquid:BTC/orderbook?depth=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const book = await client.getOrderbook('hyperliquid:BTC', { depth: 3 });
```

```python
book = client.get_orderbook("hyperliquid:BTC", depth=3)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

