```json
  "bids": [
    [
      "64432",
      "0.43796"
    ],
    [
      "64431",
      "0.00036"
    ],
    [
      "64426",
      "0.0015"
    ]
  ],
  "asks": [
    [
      "64433",
      "27.19556"
    ],
    [
      "64434",
      "2.14586"
    ],
    [
      "64435",
      "10.15654"
    ]
  ],
  "ts": 1794953722689,
  "stale": false,
  "source": "venue"
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `bids` | `array of [string, string]` | yes |  |
| `asks` | `array of [string, string]` | yes |  |
| `ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | milliseconds since the Unix epoch |
| `stale` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `source` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`unknown_instrument`](#err-unknown_instrument), [`unknown_market`](#err-unknown_market), [`unknown_venue`](#err-unknown_venue) |
| 409 | [`ambiguous_symbol`](#err-ambiguous_symbol) |
| 422 | [`no_orderbook`](#err-no_orderbook), [`venue_rejected`](#err-venue_rejected) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |
| 503 | [`stale_data`](#err-stale_data), [`venue_rate_limited`](#err-venue_rate_limited), [`venue_timeout`](#err-venue_timeout), [`venue_unavailable`](#err-venue_unavailable) |

<a id="op-getCandles"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/markets/{symbol}/candles`

**Closed bars of one market.** Operation id `getCandles`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Spot market data has fewer intervals than the venues: an interval it does not store answers 422 `unsupported_interval`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `symbol` | path | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`, `spot:ETH`); a bare symbol is accepted when it is not ambiguous — 1–120 characters |
| `interval` | query | `"1m" \| "5m" \| "15m" \| "1h" \| "4h" \| "1d"` | — | bar length — default "1h" |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…1000` | — | most recent closed bars, oldest first in the answer — default 200 |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/markets/hyperliquid:BTC/candles?interval=1h&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { candles } = await client.getCandles('hyperliquid:BTC', { interval: '1h', limit: 3 });
```

```python
candles = client.get_candles("hyperliquid:BTC", interval="1h", limit=3).candles
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json

  "interval": "1h",

    {
      "ts": 1794945600000,

      "low": "63666",
      "close": "63964",

      "volume": "3580.64025"
    }
  ],
  "ts": 1794953723670,
  "stale": false,
  "source": "venue"
}
```

| `candles[].high` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `candles[].low` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `candles[].close` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `candles[].volume` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991 \| null` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

|---|---|

| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`unknown_instrument`](#err-unknown_instrument), [`unknown_market`](#err-unknown_market), [`unknown_venue`](#err-unknown_venue) |

<a id="op-listPrivateMarkets"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/private-markets`

**Synthetic contracts on private companies, venue by venue, with sourced valuations.** Operation id `listPrivateMarkets`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Live discovery (Hyperliquid builder dexes categorised `preipo`, edgeX `pre_ipo`) joined with a reviewed contract table. Five quantities are separate fields: synthetic price, contract market value, notional exposure, implied valuation estimate (or null + reason), last known private valuation. A venue that cannot be read gives rows with `error`; the others answer. Venues are never averaged.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `delisted` | query | `"true" \| "false"` | — | true: only delisted markets (frozen prices) |
| `size` | query | `string` | — | hypothetical position size, contracts (default 1) — pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,11})(\.\d{1,8})?$ |
| `side` | query | `"long" \| "short"` | — | side of the hypothetical position (default long) |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/private-markets" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { markets, companies } = await client.listPrivateMarkets();
```

```python
private = client.list_private_markets()
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Cut to one market. Display only: `tradable` is always false.

<a id="op-getPrivateMarketCompany"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/private-markets/companies/{slug}`

**Every contract on one private company, side by side (never merged).** Operation id `getPrivateMarketCompany`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

The contracts that reference one company (`slug` from GET /private-markets, `companies`), each on its own row with its own unit and valuation breakdown, plus the delisted ones and the sourced private-valuation references. `merged` is always null: a figure of one venue is never combined with another's. Display only: none of these contracts is tradable from this wallet.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `slug` | path | `string` | yes | company slug, from GET /private-markets (`companies[].slug`), e.g. `anthropic` — pattern ^[a-z0-9-]{1,40}$ |
| `size` | query | `string` | — | hypothetical position size, contracts (default 1) — pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,11})(\.\d{1,8})?$ |
| `side` | query | `"long" \| "short"` | — | side of the hypothetical position (default long) |
| `interval` | query | `"1h" \| "1d"` | — | candle length |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | candles returned at most |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/private-markets/companies/anthropic" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const company = await client.getPrivateMarketCompany('anthropic');
```

```python
company = client.get_private_market_company("anthropic")
```

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials) |
| 404 | [`unknown_company`](#err-unknown_company) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |
| 503 | [`venue_rate_limited`](#err-venue_rate_limited), [`venue_timeout`](#err-venue_timeout), [`venue_unavailable`](#err-venue_unavailable) |

<a id="op-getPrivateMarket"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/private-markets/{id}`

**One private-company contract: definition, valuation breakdown, candles, top of book.** Operation id `getPrivateMarket`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

One contract (`id` from GET /private-markets): what it is (nature, unit, settlement, oracle), its market figures each with source, time and `stale`, the valuation breakdown for a hypothetical position (`size`, `side`), the top of the book and candles (`interval`, `limit`). An instrument that exists and is not a private-company contract answers 404 `not_a_private_market`. Display only.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol`, e.g. `hyperliquid:io:ANTH`, `edgex:ANTHROPICUSDC` — 3–120 characters · pattern ^[a-z]+:[A-Za-z0-9:._-]+$ |
| `size` | query | `string` | — | hypothetical position size, contracts (default 1) — pattern ^(0\|[1-9]\d{0,11})(\.\d{1,8})?$ |
| `side` | query | `"long" \| "short"` | — | side of the hypothetical position (default long) |
| `interval` | query | `"1h" \| "1d"` | — | candle length |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | candles returned at most |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/private-markets/hyperliquid:io:ANTH?interval=1d&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const pm = await client.getPrivateMarket('hyperliquid:io:ANTH', { interval: '1d', limit: 3 });
```

```python
pm = client.get_private_market("hyperliquid:io:ANTH", interval="1d", limit=3)
```

<a id="tag-portfolio"></a>

### Portfolio

What the wallet holds: on-chain balances, the valued portfolio with every derivatives account, and the activity feed. Scope `wallet:read`. Paper and live are separate figures, never summed.

<a id="op-getPortfolio"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/portfolio`

**Wallet value and derivative accounts, per mode.** Operation id `getPortfolio`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Wallet balances valued in USD, then every derivatives account with its `mode`. Every amount is a decimal string. Paper and live are never added together.

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/portfolio" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const portfolio = await client.getPortfolio();
```

```python
portfolio = client.get_portfolio()
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Cut to one balance. The test wallet is empty; `totals` are three separate figures, never summed.

```json
{
  "asOf": "2026-11-17T22:15:27.274Z",
  "wallet": {
    "valueUsd": "0",
    "priced": 0,
    "unpriced": 0,
    "pricesAsOf": "2026-11-17T22:15:27.274Z",
    "balances": [
      {
        "network": "ethereum",
        "networkName": "Ethereum",
        "asset": "native",
        "symbol": "ETH",
        "name": "Ether",
        "decimals": 18,

        "display": "0",

}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `asOf` | `string` | yes |  |
| `wallet` | `object` | yes |  |

| `wallet.priced` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes |  |

| `accounts[].equity` | `string \| null` | yes |  |

| `errors[].source` | `string` | yes |  |
| `errors[].message` | `string` | yes |  |

<a id="op-getBalances"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/balances`

**On-chain balances of the wallet.** Operation id `getBalances`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

One row per asset of the wallet registry, read from the chains now: `amount` is the exact integer of base units as a string, `display` the same in the asset's decimals. A network that cannot be read gives rows with `error` (and an entry in `errors`), never a zero; 503 only when no network answered. Not valued: GET /portfolio adds prices.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `network` | query | `string` | — | one network id (e.g. `base`, `arbitrum`, `solana`); default: every network of the wallet — 0–24 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/balances?network=base" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { balances } = await client.getBalances({ network: 'base' });
```

```python
balances = client.get_balances(network="base").balances
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json
    }

}
```

<a id="op-getActivity"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/activity`

**Wallet activity and fills, newest first.** Operation id `getActivity`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Sends, swaps, bridges, receipts and derivative fills, newest first, in one stable order (time, then id). Paginated: `nextCursor` of a page is the `cursor` of the next; null at the end. An item that appears after the first page was read is not inserted into the pages that follow.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | rows returned at most — default 100 |
| `cursor` | query | `string` | — | opaque position returned as `nextCursor` by the previous page; send it with the same filters — 1–400 characters · pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/activity?limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const page = await client.getActivity({ limit: 2 });
for await (const item of client.iterateActivity()) console.log(item.kind, item.at);   // every page
```

```python
page = client.get_activity(limit=2)
for item in client.iter_activity():   # every page
    print(item.kind, item.at)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json

    {

      "mode": "paper",

}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `items` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `items[].kind` | `string` | yes |  |
| `items[].at` | `string` | yes |  |
| `items[].mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null` | yes | pass it as `cursor` to read the next (older) page; null when there is none |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request), [`invalid_cursor`](#err-invalid_cursor) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |

<a id="tag-nfts"></a>

### Nfts

Read-only NFT data: the wallet's inventory (`wallet:read`) and market data of any item (`markets:read`). No transfer and no market write exists in this API. An NFT never has a value here.

<a id="op-listNfts"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/nfts`

**NFTs of the wallet (what the chain confirmed as held).** Operation id `listNfts`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

The inventory as last synchronised: Solana (Token Metadata, programmable, Token-2022, Core, compressed through an index) and the EVM networks (ERC-721, ERC-1155). Every row names the source and confidence of its ownership. NFTs carry NO value: `valuation.state` is `unavailable` (never zero, never a floor) and they are not part of GET /portfolio. Paginated: `nextCursor` of a page is the `cursor` of the next.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `network` | query | `string` | — | one network id; default: all — 0–24 characters |
| `collection` | query | `string` | — | collection key — 0–260 characters |
| `status` | query | `"in_wallet" \| "left" \| "tracked"` | — | in_wallet: held now; left: was found in the wallet and is no longer held; tracked: added by hand. Default: held, tracked, or left within the last 30 days |
| `q` | query | `string` | — | case-insensitive text matched on the name, the key or the collection name — 0–80 characters |
| `sort` | query | `"recent" \| "name"` | — | order of the list — default "recent" |
| `spam` | query | `"hide" \| "show" \| "only"` | — | items an indexer flags as spam are hidden by default, never deleted — default "hide" |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…100` | — | rows returned at most — default 48 |
| `cursor` | query | `string` | — | opaque position returned as `nextCursor` by the previous page; send it with the same filters — 1–400 characters · pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/nfts?limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { items, nextCursor } = await client.listNfts({ limit: 2 });
```

```python
nfts = client.list_nfts(limit=2)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. The test wallet holds no NFT: the list is empty, and `valuation` is always `unavailable`.

```json
{
  "items": [],
  "total": 0,
  "hiddenSpam": 0,
  "nextCursor": null,
  "valuation": {
    "state": "unavailable",
    "reason": "no_reliable_valuation",
    "note": "an NFT has no value this wallet can establish: it is not counted as zero and not valued at a collection floor; it is not part of GET /portfolio"
  },
  "asOf": "2026-11-17T22:15:28.204Z"
}
```

| `items[].tokenId` | `string \| null` | yes | decimal string (up to 2^256 − 1), never a number |
| `items[].mintAddress` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `items[].quantity` | `string` | yes | exact integer of base units, as a string — pattern ^(0\|[1-9][0-9]{0,77})$ |
| `items[].name` | `string \| null` | yes | declared by the creator — not chain truth |
| `items[].onchainName` | `string \| null` | yes |  |

<a id="op-getNft"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/nfts/item`

**One NFT of the wallet: identity, ownership, creator metadata, sources.** Operation id `getNft`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Only an NFT of the inventory (404 `unknown_nft` otherwise — a looked-up item is not the wallet's). On-chain facts, creator-declared metadata and indexed data are separate fields, each with its source. `marketActions` lists the market actions: none exists in this version.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | query | `string` | yes | `family:network:asset-id`, as returned by GET /nfts — 5–260 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/nfts/item?key=evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d%2F1" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { nft } = await client.getNft('evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1');
```

```python
nft = client.get_nft("evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1").nft
```

**Example response** — 200, written from the schema (not recorded)

<a id="op-getNftActivity"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/nfts/activity`

**On-chain events of one NFT of the wallet.** Operation id `getNftActivity`.

- **Permission:** `wallet:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Mints, transfers and burns read from the chain. A transfer is never a sale: `price` is null unless SP Wallet linked a payment of the SAME transaction through a decoded marketplace event (Seaport `OrderFulfilled`), in which case the event is a `sale` and `interpretedBy` names the decoder. `completeness` says how much history the sources gave.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | query | `string` | yes | `family:network:asset-id`, as returned by GET /nfts — 5–260 characters |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…50` | — | rows returned at most — default 20 |
| `cursor` | query | `string` | — | `nextCursor` of the previous page — 0–400 characters |
| `type` | query | `"mint" \| "transfer" \| "transfer-in" \| "transfer-out" \| "burn" \| "sale" \| "unknown"` | — | only events of this type |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/nfts/activity?key=evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d%2F1&limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { events } = await client.getNftActivity({ key: 'evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1', limit: 2 });
```

```python
events = client.get_nft_activity("evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1", limit=2).events
```

**Example response** — 200, written from the schema (not recorded). A transfer has no price: `price` is set only when a decoded marketplace event of the same transaction links a payment.

```json
{
  "key": "evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1",
  "events": [
    {
      "id": "0x5c1f…e2a9:14",
      "type": "transfer-in",
      "tx": "0x5c1f0d2b7a9e4c3f8b6a1d0e9f7c5b3a2d1e0f9c8b7a6d5e4f3c2b1a0f9ee2a9",
      "at": "2026-10-02T14:11:23.000Z",
      "from": "0x00000000000000000000000000000000000000bb",
      "to": "0x00000000000000000000000000000000000000aa",
      "quantity": "1",
      "finality": "final",
      "price": null,
      "interpretedBy": "chain"
    }
  ],
  "nextCursor": null,
  "completeness": "partial",
  "note": null,
  "error": null
}
```

|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes |  |
| `events` | `array of object` | yes |  |

| `events[].type` | `string` | yes |  |
| `events[].tx` | `string` | yes |  |
| `events[].at` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `events[].from` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `events[].to` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `events[].quantity` | `string` | yes |  |
| `events[].finality` | `string` | yes |  |
| `events[].price` | `object \| null` | yes | set ONLY when a payment of the same transaction was linked by a decoded marketplace event; a transfer alone never has one |
| `events[].interpretedBy` | `string` | yes |  |

| `error` | `string \| null` | yes |  |

<a id="op-getNftMarket"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/nfts/market`

**Market data of any NFT, one row per source.** Operation id `getNftMarket`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

The item is named by its chain identifiers and read on-chain first. Then every configured market source answers on its own row: asking price, best offer, last sale, collection floor — five different notions, each in its own currency (network + exact mint / contract + decimals), with the source, its date and `stale`. A source without its key answers `needs_key` with the variable name; nothing is ever filled in. Sources are never merged: disagreements are listed in `contradictions`. Says nothing about the wallet. `rates` are secondary; a rate from a price source licensed for internal use is withheld from an API key.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `network` | query | `string` | yes | network id, e.g. `ethereum`, `base`, `solana` — 0–24 characters |
| `address` | query | `string` | yes | EVM: the contract; Solana: the mint / asset address — 26–64 characters |
| `tokenId` | query | `string` | — | EVM: required, decimal or 0x-hex — 0–80 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/nfts/market?network=ethereum&address=0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D&tokenId=1" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const market = await client.getNftMarket({ network: 'ethereum', address: '0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D', tokenId: '1' });
```

```python
market = client.get_nft_market(network="ethereum", address="0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D", token_id="1")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Recorded on a server without a marketplace key: the source answers `needs_key` and names the variable. Nothing is filled in.

```json
{
  "key": "evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1",

  "sources": [

      "url": null,
      "listed": {
        "state": "unknown"

        "state": "unknown"
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes |  |
| `network` | `string` | yes |  |
| `standard` | `string` | yes |  |
| `sources` | `array of object` | yes | ONE ROW PER SOURCE: never merged, never averaged |
| `sources[].status` | `object` | yes |  |

| `sources[].ask` | `object` | yes | asking price of a live listing |
| `sources[].bestOffer` | `object` | yes | `known` carries `value`; `none` = established as absent; `unknown` = could not be established (`reason` may say why); `unsupported` = the standard has no such notion. Zero is a value |
| `sources[].lastSale` | `object` | yes | last sale the source reports |
| `sources[].collectionFloor` | `object` | yes | a statistic of the COLLECTION — never the value of this item |
| `sources[].verification` | `object \| null` | yes | the marketplace's own badge, named — not an on-chain verification |
| `sources[].rarity` | `object` | yes | `known` carries `value`; `none` = established as absent; `unknown` = could not be established (`reason` may say why); `unsupported` = the standard has no such notion. Zero is a value |
| `contradictions` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `contradictions[].field` | `string` | yes |  |
| `contradictions[].providers` | `array of string` | yes |  |
| `contradictions[].note` | `string` | yes |  |
| `rates` | `map of object` | yes | fiat rates of the currencies quoted, keyed `network:asset` — secondary information with its own source and date |
| `value` | `object` | yes |  |
| `value.state` | `"unavailable"` | yes |  |
| `value.reason` | `string` | yes |  |
| `marketActions` | `array of object` | yes | the market actions, none of which exists in this version: every one is `available: false` with its reason |
| `marketActions[].id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `marketActions[].available` | `false` | yes |  |
| `marketActions[].reason` | `string` | yes |  |
| `asOf` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |

<a id="op-getNftMarketEvents"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/nfts/market/events`

**Market events of any NFT (sales, listings, offers), per source.** Operation id `getNftMarketEvents`.

- **Permission:** `markets:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

What each market source reports, newest first, with the payment token and quantity when the source gives them. Filter by `type`, `after`, `before`. One list per source: a `nextCursor` is passed back as `cursor` together with `provider`. A wash-trade flag is passed through when a source has one (`unknown` otherwise). These are market statements, listed apart from the chain's transfers (GET /nfts/activity).

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `network` | query | `string` | yes | network id, e.g. `ethereum`, `base`, `solana` — 0–24 characters |
| `address` | query | `string` | yes | EVM: the contract; Solana: the mint / asset address — 26–64 characters |
| `tokenId` | query | `string` | — | EVM: required, decimal or 0x-hex — 0–80 characters |
| `provider` | query | `string` | — | only this source (its id is `sources[].status.provider`); required with `cursor` — pattern ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,30}$ |
| `type` | query | `string` | — | comma-separated: sale, list, delist, offer, cancel-offer, transfer, mint — 0–120 characters · pattern ^[a-z,-]+$ |
| `after` | query | `string` | — | only events after this instant (ISO 8601) — ISO 8601 date-time |
| `before` | query | `string` | — | only events before this instant (ISO 8601) — ISO 8601 date-time |
| `cursor` | query | `string` | — | a position in ONE source's list: send it with `provider` — 0–600 characters |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…50` | — | rows returned at most — default 20 |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/nfts/market/events?network=ethereum&address=0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D&tokenId=1&type=sale&limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { sources } = await client.getNftMarketEvents({ network: 'ethereum', address: '0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D', tokenId: '1', type: 'sale', limit: 2 });
```

```python
sources = client.get_nft_market_events(network="ethereum", address="0xBC4CA0EdA7647A8aB7C2061c2E118A18a936f13D", token_id="1", type="sale", limit=2).sources
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Recorded on a server without a marketplace key: the source answers `needs_key` and lists no event. With a key, each event carries `type`, `at`, `tx`, `from`, `to`, `quantity`, `price` (amount in base units + currency) and `flags.washTrade`.

```json
{
  "key": "evm:ethereum:0xbc4ca0eda7647a8ab7c2061c2e118a18a936f13d/1",
  "sources": [
    {
      "status": {
        "provider": "opensea",
        "name": "OpenSea",
        "state": "needs_key",
        "code": "no_api_key",
        "unlockedBy": "OPENSEA_API_KEY",
        "stale": false,
        "stamp": null,
        "attribution": null
      },
      "events": [],
      "nextCursor": null
    }
  ],
  "rates": {},
  "asOf": "2026-11-17T22:37:24.187Z"
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes |  |
| `sources` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `sources[].status` | `object` | yes |  |
| `sources[].events` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `sources[].nextCursor` | `string \| null` | yes | pass it as `cursor` to read the next (older) page; null when there is none |
| `rates` | `map of object` | yes |  |
| `asOf` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request), [`nft_bad_identifier`](#err-nft_bad_identifier) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`nft_not_found`](#err-nft_not_found), [`unknown_network`](#err-unknown_network) |
| 422 | [`nft_unsupported_chain`](#err-nft_unsupported_chain), [`nft_unsupported_standard`](#err-nft_unsupported_standard) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`nft_budget_exhausted`](#err-nft_budget_exhausted), [`nft_provider_rate_limited`](#err-nft_provider_rate_limited), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |
| 502 | [`nft_provider_bad_answer`](#err-nft_provider_bad_answer), [`nft_provider_disagreement`](#err-nft_provider_disagreement) |
| 503 | [`nft_disabled`](#err-nft_disabled), [`nft_provider_method_unsupported`](#err-nft_provider_method_unsupported), [`nft_provider_not_configured`](#err-nft_provider_not_configured), [`nft_provider_unavailable`](#err-nft_provider_unavailable), [`signer_unreachable`](#err-signer_unreachable) |

<a id="tag-positions"></a>

### Positions

Open positions, and the two ways out of one: close and reduce — strictly reduce-only market orders. Closing passes the kill switch.

<a id="op-listPositions"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/positions`

**Open positions, paper and live separated by `mode`.** Operation id `listPositions`.

- **Permission:** `positions:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Every open derivatives position of the wallet — paper accounts, Hyperliquid venue accounts, GMX (read on-chain) — each with its `mode`. `id` is what POST /positions/{id}/close and /reduce take; it contains colons: URL-encode it. A source that cannot be read is listed in `errors`; 503 only when none answered. Sizes and prices are decimal strings.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | query | `"paper" \| "live"` | — | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `venue` | query | `string` | — | venue id: hyperliquid, gmx, dydx, spot — 0–40 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/positions?mode=paper" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { positions } = await client.getPositions({ mode: 'paper' });
```

```python
positions = client.get_positions(mode="paper").positions
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. A position `id` contains colons: URL-encode it in a path (both SDKs do).

```json
{
  "positions": [
    {
      "id": "paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC",
      "mode": "paper",
      "venue": "hyperliquid",
      "network": "mainnet",
      "accountId": "6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2",
      "instrument": "hyperliquid:BTC",
      "side": "long",
      "size": "0.00155",
      "entryPrice": "64432",
      "markPrice": "64430.7",
      "liquidationPrice": null,
      "leverage": "3",
      "margin": "33.289195",
      "unrealizedPnl": "-0.002015",
      "source": "paper"
    }
  ],
  "errors": [],
  "ts": 1794953731436
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positions` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `positions[].id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `positions[].mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `positions[].venue` | `string` | yes |  |
| `positions[].accountId` | `string` | yes |  |
| `positions[].instrument` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `positions[].side` | `"long" \| "short"` | yes |  |
| `positions[].size` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `positions[].entryPrice` | `string` | yes | decimal number as a string (never a float) |
| `positions[].markPrice` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `positions[].unrealizedPnl` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `positions[].source` | `string` | yes |  |
| `errors` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `errors[].source` | `string` | yes |  |
| `errors[].message` | `string` | yes |  |
| `ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | milliseconds since the Unix epoch |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |
| 503 | [`service_unavailable`](#err-service_unavailable) |

<a id="op-closePosition"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/positions/{id}/close`

**Close a position.** Operation id `closePosition`.

- **Permission:** `positions:manage` — and a trading mandate bound to the key (403 `mandate_required` otherwise)
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

A strictly reduce-only market order on the whole position (`id` from GET /positions). Refused (422 `not_reducing`) if it would not strictly reduce. A key: the position must be on an account and an instrument of its mandate. Passes the kill switch; not read-only mode. The venue engine that executes the order can refuse it with a code of its own rules that this list does not name (`x-spw-errors-open`): the HTTP status still tells the class, and the order is in `data.order`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `slippageBps` | `integer 1…500` | — | worst acceptable price of the closing market order, in basis points from the reference price |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/positions/paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC/close" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'
```

```ts
const closed = await client.closePosition('paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC');
```

```python
closed = client.close_position("paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

<a id="op-reducePosition"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/positions/{id}/reduce`

**Reduce a position.** Operation id `reducePosition`.

- **Permission:** `positions:manage` — and a trading mandate bound to the key (403 `mandate_required` otherwise)
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

As close, on a fraction of the position (rounded down to the lot). The venue engine that executes the order can refuse it with a code of its own rules that this list does not name (`x-spw-errors-open`): the HTTP status still tells the class, and the order is in `data.order`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fraction` | `string` | yes | share of the position to close, in (0, 1] — pattern ^(0(\.\d+)?\|1(\.0+)?)$ |
| `slippageBps` | `integer 1…500` | — | worst acceptable price of the reducing market order, in basis points from the reference price |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/positions/paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC/reduce" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"fraction":"0.5"}'
```

```ts
const reduced = await client.reducePosition('paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC', { fraction: '0.5' });
```

```python
reduced = client.reduce_position("paper:hyperliquid:6a2c8351-cd79-44e2-b75a-f39280d0ebc2:hyperliquid:BTC", fraction="0.5")
```

<a id="tag-orders"></a>

### Orders

Preview, place, read and cancel orders, and the one-line command grammar. Every order passes the key, the mandate, the risk engine and the venue engine, in that order; a refusal names its gate.

<a id="op-listOrders"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/orders`

**Orders, newest first.** Operation id `listOrders`.

- **Permission:** `orders:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Every order of the wallet — placed through this API, in the app or by an agent — in one stable order (creation time, then id), newest first. `status` is the unified state; `engineStatus` is the executing engine's own word. Paginated: `nextCursor` of a page is the `cursor` of the next; null at the end. An order created after the first page was read is not inserted into the pages that follow.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | query | `"paper" \| "live"` | — | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `venue` | query | `string` | — | venue id: hyperliquid, gmx, dydx, spot — 0–40 characters |
| `open` | query | `"true" \| "false"` | — | true: only orders that are not final; false: only final orders |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | rows returned at most — default 100 |
| `cursor` | query | `string` | — | opaque position returned as `nextCursor` by the previous page; send it with the same filters — 1–400 characters · pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders?open=true&limit=5" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { orders, nextCursor } = await client.listOrders({ open: true, limit: 5 });
for await (const order of client.iterateOrders({ open: true })) console.log(order.id, order.status);   // every page
```

```python
page = client.list_orders(open=True, limit=5)
for order in client.iter_orders(open=True):   # every page
    print(order.id, order.status)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json

    {
      "id": "0x0276bab16d117c8323ee375b61b01d1d",
```

<a id="op-placeOrder"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/orders`

**Place an order.** Operation id `placeOrder`.

- **Permission:** `orders:execute` — and a trading mandate bound to the key (403 `mandate_required` otherwise)
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Idempotent on (principal, Idempotency-Key): a replay answers the same order and never sends a second request to a venue; the same key with a different order answers 409 `idempotency_conflict`.

Owner session: the order is checked and waits `AWAITING_APPROVAL` until the owner approves it with the passkey in the app (paper: executed at once, nothing is signed).

API key: needs a trading mandate bound to the key that covers the order exactly (403 `mandate_denied` with the reasons otherwise), then the risk engine, then the venue engine's mandate path — the isolated signer applies the derivative mandate it stores.

LIVE needs `SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION=on` on the server (403 `execution_disabled` otherwise) and the existing mainnet locks. Live orders with a key exist on Hyperliquid only.

A refusal after the order exists answers 4xx with the order in `data.order` (state `REJECTED` or `FAILED`). An outcome that is not known is `UNKNOWN`, never a failure.

Spot orders: 403 `spot_execution_requires_agent_vault` for a key; 422 `spot_via_swap_screen` for the owner. The venue engine that executes the order can refuse it with a code of its own rules that this list does not name (`x-spw-errors-open`): the HTTP status still tells the class, and the order is in `data.order`.

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `accountId` | `string` | — | paper account or venue account; required when the wallet has several on the venue — a UUID |
| `instrument` | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`, `spot:ETH`); a bare symbol is accepted when it is not ambiguous — 1–120 characters |
| `side` | `"buy" \| "sell"` | yes | buy opens or adds to a long (or reduces a short); sell the reverse |
| `type` | `"limit" \| "market" \| "stop_market" \| "stop_limit" \| "take_profit_market" \| "take_profit_limit"` | yes | market: immediate, bounded by `slippageBps`. limit: rests at `price`. stop_* and take_profit_*: wait for `triggerPrice`, then act as a market order or as a limit order at `price` |
| `size` | `string` | — | quantity of the base asset. Exactly one of `size` and `notional` is required — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `notional` | `string` | — | value of the order in the quote currency of the instrument (USD or a USD stablecoin on every venue of this wallet). The SERVER turns it into a size: notional / reference price, rounded DOWN to the lot size — the reference is the limit price, else the trigger price, else the mark price read now (503 `stale_data` when it is stale; 422 `below_min_size` under one lot). The answer carries the size that was used. Exactly one of `size` and `notional` is required. A SPOT order: a SELL names `size` (the quantity sold); a BUY names `notional` — the amount of the quote asset to spend — because a swap is exact-input — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `price` | `string` | — | limit price, a multiple of the instrument's `tickSize`; required by `limit`, `stop_limit` and `take_profit_limit`, refused on a market order — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `triggerPrice` | `string` | — | price at which a stop or take-profit order is triggered; required by the four trigger types — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `reduceOnly` | `boolean` | — | true: the order may only reduce an existing position — it can neither open, increase nor flip one — default false |
| `tif` | `"gtc" \| "ioc" \| "alo"` | — | time in force of a limit order: gtc = rests until cancelled, ioc = fills what it can at once and cancels the rest, alo = add liquidity only (post-only) — default "gtc" |
| `slippageBps` | `integer 1…500` | — | market orders: the worst acceptable price, in basis points from the reference price |
| `leverage` | `string` | — | paper only: leverage setting of the instrument on the account. On a venue account the leverage is a venue setting — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `maxFeeUsd` | `string` | — | highest fee accepted, USD; default: 10 bps of the notional — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `clientOrderId` | `string` | — | idempotency key of the ORDER; default: the Idempotency-Key header. The order id is derived from it and from the principal — pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{8,100}$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'
```

```ts
const placed = await client.createOrder({ mode: 'paper', instrument: 'hyperliquid:BTC', side: 'buy', type: 'market', notional: '100' }, { idempotencyKey: 'bot-2026-11-17-0001' });
```

```python
placed = client.create_order(mode="paper", instrument="hyperliquid:BTC", side="buy", order_type="market", notional="100", idempotency_key="bot-2026-11-17-0001")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. A paper market order is filled at once by the paper engine against the real Hyperliquid book: `status` is already `FILLED`.

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order` | `Order` | yes |  |
| `idempotent` | `boolean` | yes | true: this Idempotency-Key (or clientOrderId) already placed this order; nothing was sent again |
| `approval` | `object \| null` | yes | set while an owner order waits AWAITING_APPROVAL: where the owner approves it with the passkey. Always null for an API key |
| `approval.by` | `string` | yes |  |
| `approval.engine` | `string` | yes |  |
| `approval.accountId` | `string` | yes |  |
| `approval.clientId` | `string` | yes |  |
| `approval.hash` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `approval.expiresAt` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991 \| null` | yes |  |
| `approval.approve` | `object` | yes |  |

</details>

| 503 | [`authorization_unknown`](#err-authorization_unknown), [`mandate_unavailable`](#err-mandate_unavailable), [`no_liquidity`](#err-no_liquidity), [`signer_unreachable`](#err-signer_unreachable), [`stale_data`](#err-stale_data), [`unknown_instrument`](#err-unknown_instrument), [`venue_rate_limited`](#err-venue_rate_limited), [`venue_timeout`](#err-venue_timeout), [`venue_unavailable`](#err-venue_unavailable) |

<a id="op-getOrder"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/orders/{id}`

**One order and its fills.** Operation id `getOrder`.

- **Permission:** `orders:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

The order in its unified state, its fills, and `history`: every transition it went through, oldest first, with the event and the reason code. Poll this to follow an order until `final` is true (both SDKs: `waitForOrder` / `wait_for_order`); `UNKNOWN` is not final — keep reading.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { order, fills } = await client.getOrder('0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261');
const done = await client.waitForOrder('0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261');   // polls until final; UNKNOWN keeps waiting
```

```python
detail = client.get_order("0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261")
done = client.wait_for_order("0xff18683e8e2aa319d3812cf8a47fc261")   # polls until final; UNKNOWN keeps waiting
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Fills and history cut to two entries.

<a id="op-cancelOrder"></a>

#### DELETE `/api/v1/trading/orders/{id}`

**Cancel an order.** Operation id `cancelOrder`.

- **Permission:** `orders:cancel`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

A key cancels its OWN orders only (403 `not_your_order`); no trading mandate is required, and the kill switch and read-only mode never stop a cancel: it can only remove what could still execute. At the venue the order is `CANCELLED` when the venue says so. An order that is already final answers 200 with `changed: false`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X DELETE "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/0x0276bab16d117c8323ee375b61b01d1d" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)"
```

```ts
const { order, changed } = await client.cancelOrder('0x0276bab16d117c8323ee375b61b01d1d');
```

```python
cancelled = client.cancel_order("0x0276bab16d117c8323ee375b61b01d1d")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Cancelling again answers 200 with `changed: false`.

<a id="op-previewOrder"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/orders/preview`

**Preview an order: every check it would meet, and an estimate.** Operation id `previewOrder`.

- **Permission:** `orders:prepare`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none: creates, signs and sends nothing

Resolves the instrument and the account, then runs the key limits, the server locks, the trading mandate (keys and agents), the independent risk engine and the venue engine's own checks on the market as it is now.

Answers the canonical order and its hash, every check with what was observed and its limit, an estimate (fees, slippage against the real book, liquidation price) and whether an owner passkey approval would be required. Never creates, signs or sends anything; takes no Idempotency-Key.

What WOULD refuse the order is in `refusals` (HTTP 200), not an error answer.

A key previews the order it could place: a key with no trading mandate bound to it answers 403 `mandate_required` (the scope itself needs no mandate; the previewed order does). Account figures (equity, free collateral, positions) are answered only to a key that also holds `wallet:read` or `positions:read` (`account.withheld`).

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `accountId` | `string` | — | paper account or venue account; required when the wallet has several on the venue — a UUID |
| `instrument` | `string` | yes | `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`, `spot:ETH`); a bare symbol is accepted when it is not ambiguous — 1–120 characters |
| `side` | `"buy" \| "sell"` | yes | buy opens or adds to a long (or reduces a short); sell the reverse |
| `type` | `"limit" \| "market" \| "stop_market" \| "stop_limit" \| "take_profit_market" \| "take_profit_limit"` | yes | market: immediate, bounded by `slippageBps`. limit: rests at `price`. stop_* and take_profit_*: wait for `triggerPrice`, then act as a market order or as a limit order at `price` |
| `size` | `string` | — | quantity of the base asset. Exactly one of `size` and `notional` is required — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `notional` | `string` | — | value of the order in the quote currency of the instrument (USD or a USD stablecoin on every venue of this wallet). The SERVER turns it into a size: notional / reference price, rounded DOWN to the lot size — the reference is the limit price, else the trigger price, else the mark price read now (503 `stale_data` when it is stale; 422 `below_min_size` under one lot). The answer carries the size that was used. Exactly one of `size` and `notional` is required. A SPOT order: a SELL names `size` (the quantity sold); a BUY names `notional` — the amount of the quote asset to spend — because a swap is exact-input — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `price` | `string` | — | limit price, a multiple of the instrument's `tickSize`; required by `limit`, `stop_limit` and `take_profit_limit`, refused on a market order — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `triggerPrice` | `string` | — | price at which a stop or take-profit order is triggered; required by the four trigger types — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `reduceOnly` | `boolean` | — | true: the order may only reduce an existing position — it can neither open, increase nor flip one — default false |
| `tif` | `"gtc" \| "ioc" \| "alo"` | — | time in force of a limit order: gtc = rests until cancelled, ioc = fills what it can at once and cancels the rest, alo = add liquidity only (post-only) — default "gtc" |
| `slippageBps` | `integer 1…500` | — | market orders: the worst acceptable price, in basis points from the reference price |
| `leverage` | `string` | — | paper only: leverage setting of the instrument on the account. On a venue account the leverage is a venue setting — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `maxFeeUsd` | `string` | — | highest fee accepted, USD; default: 10 bps of the notional — pattern ^\d+(\.\d+)?$ |
| `clientOrderId` | `string` | — | idempotency key of the ORDER; default: the Idempotency-Key header. The order id is derived from it and from the principal — pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{8,100}$ |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/orders/preview" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"mode":"paper","instrument":"hyperliquid:BTC","side":"buy","type":"market","notional":"100"}'
```

```ts
const preview = await client.previewOrder({ mode: 'paper', instrument: 'hyperliquid:BTC', side: 'buy', type: 'market', notional: '100' });
if (!preview.wouldExecute) console.log(preview.refusals);
```

```python
preview = client.preview_order(mode="paper", instrument="hyperliquid:BTC", side="buy", order_type="market", notional="100")
if not preview.would_execute:
    print(preview.refusals)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Risk checks cut to two. `wouldExecute` is true exactly when `refusals` is empty.

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order` | `CanonicalOrder` | yes |  |
| `hash` | `string` | yes | SHA-256 of the canonical bytes, hex |
| `resolved` | `object \| null` | yes | what the canonical order leaves open; null when it could not be resolved (see `refusals`) |
| `resolved.size` | `string \| null` | yes | base units; null when the order is sized by a quote amount the venue converts (a GMX increase) |
| `resolved.worstPrice` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `resolved.notionalUsd` | `string \| null` | yes |  |
| `wouldExecute` | `boolean` | yes | true exactly when `refusals` is empty |
| `refusals` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `refusals[].stage` | `"key" \| "lock" \| "mandate" \| "risk" \| "engine"` | yes |  |
| `refusals[].code` | `string` | yes |  |
| `refusals[].message` | `string` | yes |  |
| `refusals[].reasons` | `array of string` | — |  |
| `approval` | `object` | yes |  |
| `approval.required` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `approval.by` | `"owner_passkey" \| "mandate" \| "none"` | yes |  |
| `risk` | `object` | yes |  |
| `risk.decision` | `"allow" \| "deny"` | yes |  |
| `risk.reducing` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `risk.checks` | `array of RiskCheck` | yes |  |
| `risk.derived` | `object \| null` | yes | null for a spot order: of the risk engine only the kill switch and read-only mode apply to it |
| `mandate` | `object \| null` | yes | null for the owner session, and when the key's mandate cannot be used (a `mandate` refusal then says why) |
| `mandate.id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `mandate.decision` | `"allow" \| "deny"` | yes |  |
| `mandate.reasons` | `array of string` | yes |  |
| `engine` | `object` | yes |  |
| `engine.name` | `string` | yes |  |
| `engine.risk` | `object \| null` | yes |  |
| `engine.estimate` | `object \| null` | yes | fees, slippage against the real book, liquidation price: an estimate |
| `account` | `object` | yes |  |
| `account.id` | `string \| null` | yes | null for a spot order (the wallet itself) |
| `account.venue` | `string` | yes |  |
| `account.network` | `string` | yes |  |
| `account.equity` | `string \| null` | — |  |
| `account.freeCollateral` | `string \| null` | — |  |
| `account.withheld` | `boolean` | yes | true: the key holds neither `wallet:read` nor `positions:read`, so `equity`, `freeCollateral`, the position figures of `risk.derived`, and the account figures of the risk checks, of the refusal sentences and of the estimate are omitted |
| `ts` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | yes | milliseconds since the Unix epoch |

<a id="op-runCommand"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/commands`

**Run one line of the trading command grammar.** Operation id `runCommand`.

- **Permission:** `orders:prepare`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Deterministic grammar (no model): LONG|SHORT <asset>-PERP <notional> [x<lev>] [LIMIT <px>] [SL <px>] [TP <px>] [ON <venue>], CLOSE <asset>-PERP [pct%], CANCEL ORDER <id>, CANCEL ALL, SHOW POSITIONS|BALANCES|ORDERS|RISK, STOP ALL AGENTS, KILL, and for the owner BUY/SELL (spot).

The scope checked is the one of the operation the command resolves to: an order needs `orders:execute` (`orders:prepare` with `dryRun`), CLOSE `positions:manage`, CANCEL `orders:cancel`, SHOW the matching read scope, KILL `positions:manage`.

Resolution uses the owner's stored defaults (GET/PUT /api/v1/developer/command-defaults) and nothing else: a command the defaults do not settle answers 409 `ambiguous_command` with the questions in `data.questions` and executes nothing.

`kind` says what the command resolved to: `query` (the read answer is in `data`), `control` (a cancel or the kill switch) or `order`. A spot order (BUY ETH 100 USDC, SELL ETH 0.5) is the owner's: it becomes a swap proposal approved with the passkey. The venue engine that executes the order can refuse it with a code of its own rules that this list does not name (`x-spw-errors-open`): the HTTP status still tells the class, and the order is in `data.order`.

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `text` | `string` | yes | one line of the trading command grammar, English or French: LONG BTC-PERP 500 x2, CLOSE BTC-PERP 50%, CANCEL ORDER <id>, SHOW POSITIONS, KILL … — 1–300 characters |
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | — | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. — default "paper" |
| `accountId` | `string` | — | paper account or venue account; required when the wallet has several on the venue — a UUID |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | — | true: answer the canonical order (or the control) the command resolves to, and do nothing — default false |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/commands" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"text":"LONG BTC-PERP 100","mode":"paper","dryRun":true}'
```

```ts
const r = await client.runCommand('LONG BTC-PERP 100', { mode: 'paper', dryRun: true });
if (r.kind === 'order' && !r.executed) console.log(r.order, r.hash);
```

```python
r = client.run_command("LONG BTC-PERP 100", mode="paper", dry_run=True)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. A dry run: the canonical order the line resolves to, and nothing created. Needs the owner's command defaults (see Commands).


**Response** — one of:

1. SHOW …

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"query"` | yes |  |
| `command` | `string` | yes | the parsed command type |
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `query` | `string` | yes |  |
| `data` | `object` | yes | the answer of the matching read operation |

2. CANCEL, KILL, STOP ALL AGENTS

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"control"` | yes |  |
| `command` | `string` | yes | the parsed command type |
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `executed` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `control` | `object` | yes |  |
| `result` | `object` | — | CANCEL ORDER, executed: the answer of the cancel |
| `results` | `array of object` | — | CANCEL ALL, executed: one row per order |
| `orders` | `array of string` | — | CANCEL ALL, dry run: the ids that would be cancelled |
| `killSwitch` | `Switch` | — |  |

3. an order command with `dryRun`

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"order"` | yes |  |
| `command` | `string` | yes | the parsed command type |
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `executed` | `false` | yes |  |
| `order` | `CanonicalOrder` | yes | the canonical order the command resolves to; nothing was created |
| `hash` | `string` | yes |  |
| `attached` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `preview` | `object` | — | spot: the preview of the swap the order would become |

4. an order command, executed

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"order"` | yes |  |
| `command` | `string` | yes | the parsed command type |
| `mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes | paper = simulated, live = a venue or on-chain account. The two are never merged in one figure. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `executed` | `true` | yes |  |
| `order` | `Order` | yes | the order that was placed, as POST /orders answers it |
| `canonical` | `CanonicalOrder` | yes | the canonical order that was placed |
| `hash` | `string` | yes |  |
| `idempotent` | `boolean` | yes |  |
| `approval` | `object \| null` | yes |  |
| `attached` | `array of object` | yes |  |

<a id="tag-strategies"></a>

### Strategies

The strategy library and the versions pinned to agents: register, backtest, start, stop. Research and paper sign nothing.

<a id="op-listStrategies"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/strategies`

**Strategy library and the versions pinned to agents.** Operation id `listStrategies`.

- **Permission:** `strategies:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

`library`: the strategy files installed in the laboratory, each with its `path` (what POST /strategies takes) and the SHA-256 of the file. `versions`: the versions registered on agents, with their status. A source that cannot be read is listed in `errors`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | query | `"spot" \| "perp"` | — | only spot or only perp strategies |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/strategies?kind=perp" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { library, versions } = await client.listStrategies({ kind: 'perp' });
```

```python
strategies = client.list_strategies(kind="perp")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Cut to one library entry and one version.


<a id="op-createStrategy"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/strategies`

**Register a library strategy version on an agent.** Operation id `createStrategy`.

- **Permission:** `strategies:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Pins the version by the SHA-256 of its file and queues its backtest. Registers nothing new in the library and approves nothing. `agentId` is required.

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"spot" \| "perp"` | yes | must be the kind of the agent (409 `wrong_kind` otherwise) |
| `path` | `string` | yes | library path of the strategy, from GET /strategies (`library[].path`) — 1–200 characters |
| `agentId` | `string` | — | the agent the version is registered on; required in practice (400 without it) — a UUID |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/strategies" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"kind":"perp","path":"strategies/momentum_xs_perp/2","agentId":"fa9c1fd9-a005-4825-bd67-ea180a2eb83d"}'
```

```ts
const { strategy } = await client.createStrategy({ kind: 'perp', path: 'strategies/momentum_xs_perp/2', agentId: 'fa9c1fd9-a005-4825-bd67-ea180a2eb83d' });
```

```python
strategy = client.create_strategy(kind="perp", path="strategies/momentum_xs_perp/2", agent_id="fa9c1fd9-a005-4825-bd67-ea180a2eb83d").strategy
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json
{
  "strategy": {
    "id": "450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e",
    "agentId": "fa9c1fd9-a005-4825-bd67-ea180a2eb83d",
    "sha256": "75cd4dfae65f6d55667bfaf3fe2eee2b5c98c18edbe8b43dcd8480d88262e272",
    "status": "backtesting"
  },
  "note": "registered and queued for its backtest; approval for LIVE stays the owner's"
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `strategy` | `object` | yes |  |
| `strategy.id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `strategy.agentId` | `string` | yes |  |
| `strategy.sha256` | `string` | yes |  |
| `strategy.status` | `string` | yes |  |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request), [`idempotency`](#err-idempotency), [`stale_request`](#err-stale_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`not_found`](#err-not_found) |
| 409 | [`idempotency_conflict`](#err-idempotency_conflict), [`idempotency_in_progress`](#err-idempotency_in_progress), [`wrong_kind`](#err-wrong_kind) |
| 422 | [`bad_strategy`](#err-bad_strategy) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |

<a id="op-backtestStrategy"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/backtest`

**Run the backtest of a strategy version (asynchronous job).** Operation id `backtestStrategy`.

- **Permission:** `strategies:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Answers a job id; read it with GET /jobs/{id}. The result is a BACKTEST report on the version — never a performance figure.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `symbols` | `array of string` | — | default: the agent's universe — 1–12 items |
| `interval` | `"1h" \| "1d"` | — | bar interval; default: the agent's |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/strategies/450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e/backtest" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'
```

```ts
const { job } = await client.backtestStrategy('450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e');
const done = await client.waitForJob(job.id);
```

```python
job = client.backtest_strategy("450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e").job
done = client.wait_for_job(job.id)
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

```json
{
  "job": {
    "id": "43823b3b-af8c-4c78-8527-638ea5fde77b",
    "agentId": "fa9c1fd9-a005-4825-bd67-ea180a2eb83d",
    "kind": "backtest",
    "status": "queued",
    "deadLettered": false,
    "attempts": 0,
    "maxAttempts": 2,
```

<a id="op-startStrategy"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/start`

**Start the agent a strategy version is pinned to.** Operation id `startStrategy`.

- **Permission:** `strategies:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

research and paper sign nothing. Starting a perp agent on `paper` creates its PAPER trading mandate (nothing is signed; its orders go through the unified order service, the risk engine and that mandate): the key needs `agents:manage` and may not be restricted away from the agent's venue or instruments (403 `insufficient_scope` / `restricted`), and a halted paper account answers 409 `halted`. `live` is accepted ONLY when a mandate approved by the owner with the passkey is in force for this agent — for a perp agent a TRADING mandate, which only the owner activates: a key answers 409 `mandate_required` (a key, an agent or this API can never approve a mandate). Families that cannot go live answer 409 `live_unavailable` with the reason.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | yes | research = analysis and backtests, never an order; paper = explicit simulation; live = a real venue under an owner-approved mandate. One answer never carries two modes. |
| `agentId` | `string` | — | strategy operations only: the agent, when the version id alone does not name it — a UUID |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/strategies/450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e/start" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"mode":"paper"}'
```

```ts
await client.startStrategy('450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e', { mode: 'paper' });
```

```python
client.start_strategy("450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e", mode="paper")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Only the most recent version of an agent starts, and only once its backtest is done (409 `other_version`, `no_report` otherwise).


| Field | Type | Required | Description |

<a id="op-stopStrategy"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/strategies/{id}/stop`

**Stop the agent a strategy version is pinned to.** Operation id `stopStrategy`.

- **Permission:** `strategies:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Always allowed. The safe wind-down: a live perp agent's mandate is revoked and its positions are closed with reduce-only orders; a live spot agent's mandate is revoked at the signer; simulated quotes are withdrawn.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | `string` | — | why, for the agent's journal — default "" · 0–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/strategies/450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e/stop" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"documentation example"}'
```

```ts
await client.stopStrategy('450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e', { reason: 'documentation example' });
```

```python
client.stop_strategy("450bc266-e1a9-4807-9113-7da6773ddb4e", reason="documentation example")
```

<a id="tag-agents"></a>

### Agents

Trading agents: templates, lifecycle (create, start, pause, stop), and their history read ONE mode per call (research, paper or live). An agent goes live only under its own owner-approved mandate.

<a id="op-listAgents"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents`

**Trading agents.** Operation id `listAgents`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Every agent of the wallet. Decide on `lifecycle` (active, paused, stopped) and `tradingMode` (research, paper, live); `state` and `mode` are the historical raw words, kept for compatibility. A key restricted to venues sees only the agents that trade there.

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { agents } = await client.listAgents();
```

```python
agents = client.list_agents().agents
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

<a id="op-createAgent"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/agents`

**Create an agent (from a template, or a perp agent).** Operation id `createAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Nothing is signed and no vault is derived. A spot agent is created in the owner's app (403 `owner_required`). A template whose strategy is not installed answers 409 `strategy_not_installed`.

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `"spot" \| "perp"` | — | without a template: perp (a spot agent needs a vault from the signer: owner only) |
| `template` | `string` | — | one of GET /agents/templates — 0–40 characters |
| `name` | `string` | yes | shown in the app and in the lists — 2–40 characters |
| `mission` | `string` | — | free text for people; it changes nothing the agent does — default "" · 0–400 characters |
| `universe` | `array of string` | — | asset symbols the agent trades, e.g. ["BTC"]; a fixed-universe strategy needs its exact universe (422 `universe_mismatch`); default: the template's — 1–12 items |
| `venues` | `array of "hyperliquid" \| "edgex" \| "dydx" \| "gmx"` | — | venues the agent reads; default: the template's — 1–2 items |
| `strategyPath` | `string` | — | bar templates: which of the template's installed strategies to bind, from GET /agents/templates — 0–60 characters |
| `interval` | `"1h" \| "4h" \| "1d"` | — | bar agents: bar length; default 1h |
| `capitalUsdc` | `string` | — | simulated capital of the agent, a decimal string; default 1000 — pattern ^\d{1,7}(\.\d{1,6})?$ |
| `risk` | `map of number \| string \| boolean` | — | overrides of the agent's own risk limits, by name (the limits in force are in GET /agents/{id}: `riskLimits`) |
| `config` | `map of any` | — | overrides of the template's parameters, by name |
| `mode` | `"research" \| "paper"` | — | research (default): the agent is created and not running. paper: an agent created from a stream or supervisor template is started on paper at once; a bar agent (momentum, mean reversion, …, or `kind: perp`) is created and NOT started — it needs a backtested strategy version first, then POST /agents/{id}/start. `live` is refused (400 `bad_request`): an agent goes live only through POST /agents/{id}/start, under a mandate the owner approved. — default "research" |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"template":"funding-arb","name":"Docs funding","mode":"research"}'
```

```ts
const { agent } = await client.createAgent({ template: 'funding-arb', name: 'Docs funding', mode: 'research' });
```

```python
agent = client.create_agent(template="funding-arb", name="Docs funding", mode="research").agent
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

<a id="op-listAgentTemplates"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/templates`

**The seven built-in agents: family, engine, modes, installed strategies.** Operation id `listAgentTemplates`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

A bar template is bound BY NAME to laboratory strategies; `strategyInstalled: false` means none of them is installed (no placeholder is ever substituted). `liveReason` says why a family cannot go live.

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/templates" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { templates } = await client.listAgentTemplates();
```

```python
templates = client.list_agent_templates().templates
```
