<a id="op-getAgent"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}`

**One agent: model, strategy, limits, mandate reference, backtest reports (labelled), worker state.** Operation id `getAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

`agent.state` is active, paused or stopped and `agent.mode` research, paper or live. `mandateDraft` is the mandate the owner would have to approve for this agent to go live (null when none can be drafted). An unknown id, or an agent a restricted key may not see, answers 404 `not_found`.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { agent } = await client.getAgent('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b');
```

```python
agent = client.get_agent("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b").agent
```

<a id="op-listAgentSignals"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/signals`

**Signal history of one agent, ONE mode.** Operation id `listAgentSignals`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Each signal carries the mode, the configuration version, the strategy hash, a digest of its inputs, its output and the decision: `acted`, or `skipped` with a reason code.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |
| `mode` | query | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | — | default: the mode the agent is in NOW (for a stopped agent, the one it last ran in) — the answer always says which in `mode`. Name it to read one mode whatever the agent is doing; both SDKs require it. |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | rows returned at most — default 100 |
| `before` | query | `integer 0…9007199254740991` | — | unix ms: rows strictly before it |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/signals?mode=paper&limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { signals } = await client.listAgentSignals('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { mode: 'paper', limit: 2 });
```

```python
signals = client.list_agent_signals("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", mode="paper", limit=2).signals
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server

<a id="op-listAgentJournal"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/journal`

**Activity journal of one agent, ONE mode.** Operation id `listAgentJournal`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Decisions, orders, fills, errors, risk denials, mandate events and state changes, newest first.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |
| `mode` | query | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | — | default: the mode the agent is in NOW (for a stopped agent, the one it last ran in) — the answer always says which in `mode`. Name it to read one mode whatever the agent is doing; both SDKs require it. |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | rows returned at most — default 100 |
| `before` | query | `integer 0…9007199254740991` | — | unix ms: rows strictly before it |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/journal?mode=paper&limit=3" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { entries } = await client.listAgentJournal('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { mode: 'paper', limit: 3 });
```

```python
entries = client.list_agent_journal("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", mode="paper", limit=3).entries
```

<a id="op-listAgentOrders"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/orders`

**Order history of one agent, ONE mode, in the unified order states.** Operation id `listAgentOrders`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

The agent's orders in one mode, newest first, with `state` in the twelve unified order states. Page with `before` (the `ts` of the last row) and `limit`. Research places no order: its list is empty.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |
| `mode` | query | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | — | default: the mode the agent is in NOW (for a stopped agent, the one it last ran in) — the answer always says which in `mode`. Name it to read one mode whatever the agent is doing; both SDKs require it. |
| `limit` | query | `integer 1…500` | — | rows returned at most — default 100 |
| `before` | query | `integer 0…9007199254740991` | — | unix ms: rows strictly before it |
| `open` | query | `"true" \| "false"` | — | true: only the orders that are not final |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/orders?mode=paper&limit=2" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { orders } = await client.listAgentOrders('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { mode: 'paper', limit: 2 });
```

```python
orders = client.list_agent_orders("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", mode="paper", limit=2).orders
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. The recorded agent had placed no order yet.

```json
{
  "mode": "paper",
  "orders": []
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | yes | research = analysis and backtests, never an order; paper = explicit simulation; live = a real venue under an owner-approved mandate. One answer never carries two modes. |
| `orders` | `array of object` | yes |  |
| `orders[].id` | `string` | yes |  |
| `orders[].mode` | `"paper" \| "live"` | yes |  |
| `orders[].instrument` | `string` | yes |  |
| `orders[].side` | `string` | yes |  |
| `orders[].state` | `string` | yes | unified order state (CREATED … FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, FAILED, UNKNOWN) |

</details>

**Errors**

| Status | Codes |
|---|---|
| 400 | [`bad_request`](#err-bad_request) |
| 401 | [`unauthenticated`](#err-unauthenticated) |
| 403 | [`insufficient_scope`](#err-insufficient_scope), [`ip_not_allowed`](#err-ip_not_allowed), [`mixed_credentials`](#err-mixed_credentials), [`restricted`](#err-restricted) |
| 404 | [`not_found`](#err-not_found) |
| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |

<a id="op-getAgentPerformance"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/performance`

**Computed performance of one agent, ONE mode.** Operation id `getAgentPerformance`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Equity curve, returns, volatility, Sharpe, Sortino, max drawdown, win rate, profit factor, turnover, fees, funding, slippage, net PnL — computed from the records of that mode only, with small-sample warnings. Paper and live are never added. Backtests are not here.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |
| `mode` | query | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | — | default: the mode the agent is in NOW (for a stopped agent, the one it last ran in) — the answer always says which in `mode`. Name it to read one mode whatever the agent is doing; both SDKs require it. |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/performance?mode=paper" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const perf = await client.getAgentPerformance('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { mode: 'paper' });
```

```python
perf = client.get_agent_performance("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", mode="paper")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. A figure that cannot be computed is `{ value: null, reason }`, never zero.

<a id="op-getJob"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/jobs/{id}`

**One agent job (backtest, wind-down): status and result.** Operation id `getJob`.

- **Permission:** `agents:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

Poll until `job.status` is `done`, `failed` or `cancelled` (both SDKs: `waitForJob` / `wait_for_job`). A backtest's `result` is labelled as a backtest and carries its own warnings (too few data points, missing snapshots).

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/jobs/72526d3e-9b2a-4f86-977b-07b6531868a8" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const { job } = await client.getJob('72526d3e-9b2a-4f86-977b-07b6531868a8');
```

```python
job = client.get_job("72526d3e-9b2a-4f86-977b-07b6531868a8").job
```

<a id="op-startAgent"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/start`

**Start an agent in research, paper or live mode.** Operation id `startAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

research and paper sign nothing. Starting a perp agent on `paper` creates its PAPER trading mandate (nothing is signed; its orders go through the unified order service, the risk engine and that mandate): the key needs `agents:manage` and may not be restricted away from the agent's venue or instruments (403 `insufficient_scope` / `restricted`), and a halted paper account answers 409 `halted`. `live` is accepted ONLY when a mandate approved by the owner with the passkey is in force for this agent — for a perp agent a TRADING mandate, which only the owner activates: a key answers 409 `mandate_required` (a key, an agent or this API can never approve a mandate). Families that cannot go live answer 409 `live_unavailable` with the reason.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `"research" \| "paper" \| "live"` | yes | research = analysis and backtests, never an order; paper = explicit simulation; live = a real venue under an owner-approved mandate. One answer never carries two modes. |
| `agentId` | `string` | — | strategy operations only: the agent, when the version id alone does not name it — a UUID |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/start" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"mode":"paper"}'
```

```ts
await client.startAgent('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { mode: 'paper' });
```

```python
client.start_agent("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", mode="paper")
```

<a id="op-pauseAgent"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/pause`

**Pause an agent.** Operation id `pauseAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

No new exposure. A live perp agent keeps its positions and its mandate; a live spot agent's mandate is revoked at the signer (resuming it needs a new owner approval).

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | `string` | — | why, for the agent's journal — default "" · 0–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/pause" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"documentation example"}'
```

```ts
await client.pauseAgent('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { reason: 'documentation example' });
```

```python
client.pause_agent("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", reason="documentation example")
```

<a id="op-stopAgent"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/stop`

**Stop an agent (always allowed).** Operation id `stopAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Always allowed. The safe wind-down: a live perp agent's mandate is revoked and its positions are closed with reduce-only orders; a live spot agent's mandate is revoked at the signer; simulated quotes are withdrawn.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | `string` | — | why, for the agent's journal — default "" · 0–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/stop" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"documentation example"}'
```

```ts
await client.stopAgent('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { reason: 'documentation example' });
```

```python
client.stop_agent("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", reason="documentation example")
```

<a id="op-backtestAgent"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/agents/{id}/backtest`

**Run a backtest for an agent (asynchronous job).** Operation id `backtestAgent`.

- **Permission:** `agents:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** required (8–100 characters). The same key with the same request returns the first answer; nothing runs twice
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Bar agents: the laboratory backtest of the strategy version. Stream agents: the rule replayed on the top-of-book snapshots the stream engine recorded. Always a BACKTEST, labelled as such.

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | `string` | yes | the `id` a list or a creation of this API returned; URL-encode it (a position id contains colons) — 1–200 characters |

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `versionId` | `string` | — | bar agents: the strategy version to backtest; default: the agent's current one — a UUID |
| `hours` | `integer 1…168` | — | stream agents: how many hours of recorded snapshots to replay |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/agents/b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b/backtest" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"hours":1}'
```

```ts
const { job } = await client.backtestAgent('b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b', { hours: 1 });
```

```python
job = client.backtest_agent("b9180e9d-3977-403c-a072-9af105bfd59b", hours=1).job
```

<a id="tag-risk"></a>

### Risk

The state of the risk engine and of the server locks, and the one control a key has: engaging the global kill switch (never releasing it).

<a id="op-getRisk"></a>

#### GET `/api/v1/trading/risk`

**Risk state: kill switch, signer limits, capability locks, account policies.** Operation id `getRisk`.

- **Permission:** `positions:read`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** none (a read)

What can stop an order, in one read. `engine.killSwitch` and `engine.readOnly`: the global switches of the risk engine. `killSwitch`: the signer's emergency halt (a different thing). `capabilityLocks`: the server locks, each with `on` and `why` — the lock of live execution through this API (`SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION`) is NOT in this list: a preview of a live order shows it as a `lock` refusal. `accounts`: the policy of every derivatives account. `signerPolicy`: the signer's per-transaction and daily limits.

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/risk" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY"
```

```ts
const risk = await client.getRisk();
if (risk.engine.killSwitch.on) console.log('no new risk until the owner releases it');
```

```python
risk = client.get_risk()
if risk.engine.kill_switch.on:
    print("no new risk until the owner releases it")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. Cut to one lock and one account; the per-asset maps of the signer policy are cut to four entries.

<a id="op-engageKillSwitch"></a>

#### POST `/api/v1/trading/risk/kill-switch`

**Engage the global kill switch.** Operation id `engageKillSwitch`.

- **Permission:** `positions:manage`
- **Idempotency-Key:** not used
- **Side effects:** yes — see the description

Blocks every NEW risk for every principal on every venue and in every mode; cancels and strictly reducing orders still pass. Engaging needs no mandate (it only removes risk) and takes no Idempotency-Key: engaging a switch that is on changes nothing. Releasing it is NOT possible with a key: owner session and passkey only.

**Request body** (JSON; unknown fields are refused)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reason` | `string` | — | why, for the audit trail and the `risk.kill_switch` webhook — default "" · 0–200 characters |

**Example request**

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$SP_WALLET_BASE_URL/api/v1/trading/risk/kill-switch" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SP_WALLET_API_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"reason":"documentation example"}'
```

```ts
await client.engageKillSwitch('bot detected a runaway loop');
```

```python
client.engage_kill_switch("bot detected a runaway loop")
```

**Example response** — 200, recorded from a real server. A key can engage it and can never release it.

```json
{
  "killSwitch": {
    "on": true,
    "at": 1794953765956,
    "by": {
      "kind": "api_key",
      "id": "spk_0123456789ab"
    },
    "reason": "documentation example"
  },
  "readOnly": {
    "on": false,
    "at": null,
    "by": null,
    "reason": null
  }
}
```

<details><summary><b>Response fields</b></summary>

|---|---|---|---|
| `killSwitch` | `Switch` | yes |  |
| `readOnly` | `Switch` | yes |  |

</details>

| 429 | [`auth_throttled`](#err-auth_throttled), [`rate_limited`](#err-rate_limited) |
| 500 | [`internal`](#err-internal) |

<a id="objects"></a>

## Objects

Shapes the answers share (`#/components/schemas/<name>` in the OpenAPI document).

<a id="schema-Error"></a>

### Error

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `error` | `string` | yes | stable machine code: one of the operation's `x-spw-errors` |
| `message` | `string` | yes | human-readable sentence (English) |
| `candidates` | `array of string` | — | `ambiguous_symbol`: the `venue:symbol` ids the bare symbol could mean |
| `data` | `object` | — | machine-readable detail of a refusal |
| `data.order` | `Order` | — | the order the refusal is about, when one exists (state REJECTED or FAILED) |
| `data.reasons` | `array of string` | — | `mandate_denied`: codes of `x-spw-mandate-deny-reasons`; a mandate that cannot be used: the error code in upper case |
| `data.failed` | `array of string` | — | `risk_denied`, `kill_switch_active`, `read_only_mode`: ids of the failed risk checks |
| `data.checks` | `array of RiskCheck` | — | the failed risk checks, each with what was observed and its limit |
| `data.decisionId` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | — |  |
| `data.questions` | `array of object` | — | `ambiguous_command`: what the command and the owner's defaults leave open |
| `data.code` | `string` | — | `parse_error`: the grammar's own code |
| `data.token` | `integer -9007199254740991…9007199254740991` | — | `parse_error`: index of the offending token |

Final: FILLED, CANCELLED, REJECTED, FAILED. UNKNOWN means the outcome at the venue is not known yet: it is not final and it is not a failure |

<a id="error-catalogue"></a>

## Error catalogue

Every code an operation of this API can answer (153), with what it means and what to do. The `message` of a real answer carries the figures of the case.

| Code | Status | Meaning | What to do |
|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="err-account_has_active_mandate"></a>`account_has_active_mandate` | 409 | This venue account already carries the mandate of another principal: one venue account serves one principal at a time. | Use another account, or the owner revokes the other mandate. |
| <a id="err-account_required"></a>`account_required` | 409 | The wallet has several accounts on this venue in this mode. | Name one with `accountId` (GET /portfolio lists them). |
| <a id="err-ambiguous_command"></a>`ambiguous_command` | 409 | The command line and the owner's stored defaults leave something open (venue, network, leverage, margin mode, fee cap, order validity). Nothing was executed. | Read `data.questions` (each has a `field` and its `options`). Say it in the command (`x2`, `ON hyperliquid`) or have the owner set the defaults once. |
| <a id="err-ambiguous_order"></a>`ambiguous_order` | 409 | The order and the owner's defaults do not settle everything (spot). | Read `data.questions`; name what is missing, or the owner sets the command defaults. |
| <a id="err-ambiguous_position"></a>`ambiguous_position` | 409 | The wallet holds a long and a short on this GMX market. | Name the position by its key. |
| <a id="err-ambiguous_symbol"></a>`ambiguous_symbol` | 409 | A bare symbol (`BTC`) exists on several venues. | Name one of `candidates` as `venue:symbol` (e.g. `hyperliquid:BTC`). |
| <a id="err-auth_throttled"></a>`auth_throttled` | 429 | Ten failed authentications in a minute from this address: every key request from it is refused for the rest of the window, valid keys included. | Stop sending a bad key. Wait 60 seconds (`Retry-After`). |
| <a id="err-authorization_expired"></a>`authorization_expired` | 422 | The venue authorization of the account has expired. | The owner approves a new one with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-authorization_unknown"></a>`authorization_unknown` | 503 | The signer gave no usable answer: it may or may not have stored the authorization. | The owner uses "recover" on the venue account. |
| <a id="err-bad_input"></a>`bad_input` | 422 | The engine's risk context could not be evaluated. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-bad_mark"></a>`bad_mark` | 422 | The mark price is not positive. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-bad_order"></a>`bad_order` | 422 | The engine cannot express the order (e.g. too many decimals for the asset). | Fix the request; sending it again unchanged gives the same answer. |
| <a id="err-bad_policy"></a>`bad_policy` | 400, 422 | The risk policy of the account is invalid, or names an instrument that is not in the pinned asset table. | Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-bad_report"></a>`bad_report` | 409 | The backtest report disqualifies this version: it cannot run, even on paper. | Register another version. |
| <a id="err-bad_request"></a>`bad_request` | 400, 409, 422 | The path parameters, the query or the JSON body do not match the operation's schema (an unknown field is refused: bodies are strict), or a value is out of its range. | Fix the request; sending it again unchanged gives the same answer. The schema of each operation is in this reference and in the OpenAPI document. |
| <a id="err-bad_state"></a>`bad_state` | 409 | The object is not in a state that allows this: an order being handed to its engine right now, a swap already approved, an agent or job already in that state. | Read the object again; for an order, ask again in a moment. |
| <a id="err-bad_strategy"></a>`bad_strategy` | 422 | The laboratory refused the strategy file. | Read `message`; pick a path from GET /strategies (`library`). |
| <a id="err-below_min_notional"></a>`below_min_notional` | 422 | The order is worth less than the venue's minimum. | Raise it above `minNotional`. |
| <a id="err-below_min_size"></a>`below_min_size` | 422 | The size is under one lot, or the `notional` buys less than one lot at the reference price, or the fraction of a position is less than one lot. | Raise the size/notional. `lotSize`, `minOrderSize` and `minNotional` are in GET /markets/{symbol}. |
| <a id="err-cancel_in_app"></a>`cancel_in_app` | 422 | This order is cancelled in the app (a spot agent's swap, a GMX order). | Do it in the app. |
| <a id="err-close_in_app"></a>`close_in_app` | 422 | A GMX position is closed by a wallet transaction approved with the passkey. | Do it in the app. |
| <a id="err-daily_loss_exceeded"></a>`daily_loss_exceeded` | 422 | The account's daily loss limit is reached. | No new risk today; reductions pass. |
| <a id="err-data_mismatch"></a>`data_mismatch` | 422 | The ticker or book the engine holds is for another instrument. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-duplicate_client_id"></a>`duplicate_client_id` | 422 | This client order id was already used on the account. | Use a new `clientOrderId` / Idempotency-Key for a new order. |
| <a id="err-execution_disabled"></a>`execution_disabled` | 403 | Live execution through the trading API is closed on this server (`SPW_TRADING_API_EXECUTION` is not `on`). Nothing was created. | Paper is not affected; cancels and strictly reducing orders still pass. The server operator opens the lock. |
| <a id="err-execution_not_wired"></a>`execution_not_wired` | 501 | The operation exists in the contract and is not connected on this server (or, for STOP ALL AGENTS, could stop nothing from here). Never a success: nothing was done. | Do not retry. Use the operation the message points to. |
| <a id="err-exists"></a>`exists` | 409 | A risk-management (supervisor) agent already exists. | Stop it before creating another. |
| <a id="err-fee_cap_not_expressible"></a>`fee_cap_not_expressible` | 422 | The quote asset is not one the owner's defaults count as USD, so the fee cap cannot be expressed. | Use a USD quote, or the owner adds it to the defaults (`usdQuotes`). |
| <a id="err-gmx_disabled"></a>`gmx_disabled` | 403 | GMX live trading is switched off (server lock or owner setting). | Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-gmx_leverage_required"></a>`gmx_leverage_required` | 422 | A GMX increase must name its `leverage` (GMX has no leverage setting to fall back on). | Owner orders only: add `leverage`. |
| <a id="err-gmx_requires_owner_passkey"></a>`gmx_requires_owner_passkey` | 403 | Every GMX order is a wallet transaction the owner approves with the passkey: a key or an agent cannot place, close or cancel one. | Do it in the app. |
| <a id="err-gross_exposure_exceeded"></a>`gross_exposure_exceeded` | 422 | The resulting gross exposure exceeds the account policy. | Reduce first, or send a smaller order. |
| <a id="err-halted"></a>`halted` | 409, 422, 423 | 423/422: the signer's emergency halt is active, or the account is halted. 409 (agents): the agent's paper account is halted. | Cancels and reduce-only exits are still signed. The halt is lifted on the signer machine only. |
| <a id="err-idempotency"></a>`idempotency` | 400 | A mutating call without a valid `Idempotency-Key` header (8 to 100 characters: letters, digits, `.` `_` `:` `-`). | Send the header. One key per logical action; keep it for retries. |
| <a id="err-idempotency_conflict"></a>`idempotency_conflict` | 409 | This Idempotency-Key (or `clientOrderId`) was already used with a DIFFERENT request, or belongs to another principal. | A new action needs a new key. To retry the first action, send the first request again, byte for byte. |
| <a id="err-idempotency_in_progress"></a>`idempotency_in_progress` | 409 | The first call with this Idempotency-Key is still running, or just ended without an answer. | Wait a second and send the same call again. |
| <a id="err-inconsistent_prices"></a>`inconsistent_prices` | 422 | The stop-loss / take-profit are on the wrong side of the entry or of each other. | A long: SL below entry, TP above. A short: the reverse. |
| <a id="err-instrument_halted"></a>`instrument_halted` | 422 | The venue has halted this instrument. | Nothing to do until the venue reopens it. |
| <a id="err-instrument_mismatch"></a>`instrument_mismatch` | 422 | The account is on one venue and the instrument on another. | Name the account of the instrument's venue. |
| <a id="err-instrument_not_allowed"></a>`instrument_not_allowed` | 422 | The instrument is not allowed by the account's authorization or policy. | Trade an allowed instrument, or the owner widens the policy. |
| <a id="err-instrument_reduce_only"></a>`instrument_reduce_only` | 422 | The venue only accepts orders that reduce a position on this instrument. | Send reduce-only orders. |
| <a id="err-insufficient_collateral"></a>`insufficient_collateral` | 422 | Not enough free collateral on the account for this order. | Send a smaller order, or the owner adds collateral to the account. |
| <a id="err-insufficient_scope"></a>`insufficient_scope` | 403 | The key does not hold the permission (scope) this operation needs. For POST /commands: the scope of the operation the command resolves to. | Use a key that holds the scope named in `message`. Adding a scope to a key needs the owner's passkey. |
| <a id="err-internal"></a>`internal` | 500 | The server failed. A mutating call may or may not have run. | Send the same call again with the SAME Idempotency-Key: it either returns the stored answer or runs once. Do not create a new key for a retry. |
| <a id="err-invalid_cursor"></a>`invalid_cursor` | 400 | The `cursor` is not one a previous page of this list returned. | Start again without a cursor; send a cursor with the same filters it came with. |
| <a id="err-invalid_order"></a>`invalid_order` | 400, 422 | The order is not well-formed for its type: a limit without `price`, a stop without `triggerPrice`, `reduceOnly` on spot, a research-mode order, a mandate or principal that is not the caller's. | Fix the request; sending it again unchanged gives the same answer. |
| <a id="err-ip_not_allowed"></a>`ip_not_allowed` | 403 | The key has an IP allow-list and the request came from another address. | Call from an allowed address, or have the owner change the key's restrictions. |
| <a id="err-key_limit_exceeded"></a>`key_limit_exceeded` | 403 | The order is above the key's own notional limit (per order or per day), or cannot be valued so the limit cannot be checked. | Send a smaller order; the limits are on the key (Developer → Keys). |
| <a id="err-kill_switch_active"></a>`kill_switch_active` | 423 | The global kill switch is on: no new risk for anybody, on any venue, in any mode. | Cancels and strictly reducing orders still pass. Only the owner releases it, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-leverage_exceeded"></a>`leverage_exceeded` | 422 | The leverage is above the authorized maximum (policy, mandate or venue). | Lower it. |
| <a id="err-leverage_mismatch"></a>`leverage_mismatch` | 422 | A live order names a leverage that is not the venue's current setting for the instrument. | Send the order without `leverage`, or the owner changes the setting in the app. |
| <a id="err-liquidation_too_close"></a>`liquidation_too_close` | 422 | The estimated liquidation price after the order is closer to the mark than the policy allows. | Smaller size or lower leverage. |
| <a id="err-live_not_available"></a>`live_not_available` | 422 | A paper account can only be PAPER or READ_ONLY. | Use a venue account for live. |
| <a id="err-live_unavailable"></a>`live_unavailable` | 409 | This agent family cannot go live (the message says why). | Run it on paper. |
| <a id="err-mainnet_disabled"></a>`mainnet_disabled` | 403 | The server lock for new mainnet risk is closed (`SPW_DERIV_LIVE_MAINNET`). | Paper works; cancels and reductions pass. Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-mandate_denied"></a>`mandate_denied` | 403 | The order is outside the mandate. `data.reasons` lists EVERY reason (codes of the mandate table, e.g. `ORDER_NOTIONAL_EXCEEDED`, `INSTRUMENT_NOT_ALLOWED`); `data.order` is the order, state REJECTED. | Change the order to fit the mandate; a wider mandate is a new mandate approved by the owner. |
| <a id="err-mandate_integrity"></a>`mandate_integrity` | 403 | The stored mandate no longer hashes to what the owner's passkey approved. It is unusable and gets revoked. | The owner approves a new mandate. |
| <a id="err-mandate_mainnet_disabled"></a>`mandate_mainnet_disabled` | 403 | The server lock for mandates on mainnet is closed (`SPW_DERIV_MANDATE_MAINNET`). | Paper works; cancels and reductions pass. Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-mandate_mismatch"></a>`mandate_mismatch` | 422 | The mandate was approved for another strategy version or another agent. | The owner approves a mandate for this one. |
| <a id="err-mandate_none"></a>`mandate_none` | 422 | Live: no derivative mandate is in force at the signer for this venue account. | The owner approves a live mandate with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-mandate_not_approved"></a>`mandate_not_approved` | 403 | The mandate bound to this principal was never approved, or is no longer active. | The owner approves a mandate and binds it. |
| <a id="err-mandate_required"></a>`mandate_required` | 403, 409 | 403: an execution scope is inert on this key because no trading mandate is bound to it. 409 (agents): going live needs a mandate the owner approved for that agent. | The owner creates and approves a mandate for the key (Settings → Risk → Trading mandates), then binds it to the key. See Mandates. |
| <a id="err-mandate_unavailable"></a>`mandate_unavailable` | 503 | Live mandates are not available on this server (a server lock). | Paper works. Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-mandate_wind_down"></a>`mandate_wind_down` | 422 | The mandate was revoked or has expired: for 24 h only cancels and reduce-only orders are placed. | Reduce or close; new risk needs a new mandate. |
| <a id="err-mark_index_divergence"></a>`mark_index_divergence` | 422 | Mark and index prices are further apart than the policy allows. | Retry later. |
| <a id="err-markets_unavailable"></a>`markets_unavailable` | 503 | No market source at all could be read. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-missing_balance"></a>`missing_balance` | 422 | A percentage of a balance was asked and the balance could not be read. | Retry, or name an absolute quantity. |
| <a id="err-missing_reference_price"></a>`missing_reference_price` | 422 | No USD reference price is available to express the fee cap. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-mixed_credentials"></a>`mixed_credentials` | 403 | The request carried an API key and a session cookie (or a stream ticket) together. | Send exactly one credential. A script never sends the cookie. |
| <a id="err-mode_read_only"></a>`mode_read_only` | 422 | The venue account is read-only: live trading was never authorized with the owner's passkey, or the authorization was revoked. | The owner authorizes the account in Futures → Accounts. |
| <a id="err-nft_bad_identifier"></a>`nft_bad_identifier` | 400 | The address or token id is not well-formed for the network. | Fix the request; sending it again unchanged gives the same answer. |
| <a id="err-nft_budget_exhausted"></a>`nft_budget_exhausted` | 429 | The wallet's own request budget for a metered provider is spent for now. | Retry later; do not poll. |
| <a id="err-nft_disabled"></a>`nft_disabled` | 503 | The NFT module is switched off on this server. | The server operator enables it. |
| <a id="err-nft_not_found"></a>`nft_not_found` | 404 | The chain does not know this item. | Check network, address and token id. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_bad_answer"></a>`nft_provider_bad_answer` | 502 | A provider answered something that could not be interpreted. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_disagreement"></a>`nft_provider_disagreement` | 502 | Sources disagree on a fact the answer needs. | Read GET /nfts/market: each source is on its own row. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_method_unsupported"></a>`nft_provider_method_unsupported` | 503 | The provider cannot answer this kind of question for this network. | Nothing to do. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_not_configured"></a>`nft_provider_not_configured` | 503 | The provider this call needs has no key on the server. | The answer names the server variable; the operator sets it. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_rate_limited"></a>`nft_provider_rate_limited` | 429 | A market-data provider is rate-limiting this wallet. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-nft_provider_unavailable"></a>`nft_provider_unavailable` | 503 | A provider is down. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-nft_unsupported_chain"></a>`nft_unsupported_chain` | 422 | No NFT adapter for this network. | Use Solana or one of the EVM networks. |
| <a id="err-nft_unsupported_standard"></a>`nft_unsupported_standard` | 422 | The contract is not an NFT standard this wallet reads. | Nothing to do. |
| <a id="err-no_account"></a>`no_account` | 422 | No paper account (mode `paper`) or no authorized venue account (mode `live`) on this venue. | The owner creates a paper account, or authorizes a venue account with the passkey, in Futures → Accounts. |
| <a id="err-no_client_id_format"></a>`no_client_id_format` | 422 | The venue has no client-order-id format this wallet can derive. | Use a venue the wallet trades. |
| <a id="err-no_instrument"></a>`no_instrument` | 422 | The command names an asset no instrument of the catalogue matches (on that venue/network). | Check the asset and the venue. |
| <a id="err-no_liquidity"></a>`no_liquidity` | 503 | The book has no bid/ask from which to derive a price for a market order. | Retry later, or send a limit order. |
| <a id="err-no_orderbook"></a>`no_orderbook` | 422 | The venue has no order book (GMX is a liquidity pool; spot market data stores bars). | Use GET /markets/{symbol} for the price. |
| <a id="err-no_position"></a>`no_position` | 422 | There is no open position to close or reduce on that instrument and account. | Read GET /positions first. |
| <a id="err-no_report"></a>`no_report` | 409 | The strategy version has no backtest report. | Run POST /strategies/{id}/backtest and wait for the job. |
| <a id="err-no_strategy"></a>`no_strategy` | 409 | The agent has no approved strategy version. | Register one with POST /strategies and let its backtest finish. |
| <a id="err-not_a_private_market"></a>`not_a_private_market` | 404 | The instrument exists and is not a private-company contract. | Read it with GET /markets/{symbol}. |
| <a id="err-not_found"></a>`not_found` | 404 | Agents: no such agent, strategy version or job (or the key is restricted away from it). Any other path: not an operation of this API. | Check the id; list with GET /agents or GET /strategies. For a path, see the operation list. |
| <a id="err-not_reducing"></a>`not_reducing` | 422 | The order would not strictly reduce an existing position: nothing was placed. | Read GET /positions; the position may already be closed. |
| <a id="err-not_your_order"></a>`not_your_order` | 403 | A key cancels its own orders only. | The owner cancels anything, in the app. |
| <a id="err-order_notional_exceeded"></a>`order_notional_exceeded` | 422 | The order is above the account policy's notional per order. | Send a smaller order. |
| <a id="err-order_rate_day"></a>`order_rate_day` | 422 | Too many orders on this account today (account policy). | Wait for the next day; Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-order_rate_minute"></a>`order_rate_minute` | 422 | Too many orders on this account in the last minute (account policy). | Slow down. |
| <a id="err-other_version"></a>`other_version` | 409 | Only the most recent version of an agent can be started (or: the agent runs another approved version). | Register the version again, or start the current one. |
| <a id="err-owner_required"></a>`owner_required` | 403 | Only the owner can do this: create a spot agent (its vault is derived by the signer), let the supervisor act on live agents. | Do it in the app. |
| <a id="err-owner_session_required"></a>`owner_session_required` | 403 | A spot order (a swap proposal) is prepared and withdrawn in the owner's session, not with a key. | Do it in the app. |
| <a id="err-paper_unsupported"></a>`paper_unsupported` | 422 | The venue has no order book, so the paper engine cannot fill against it (GMX). | Paper-trade on Hyperliquid. |
| <a id="err-parse_error"></a>`parse_error` | 400 | The text is not a sentence of the command grammar. `data.code` is the grammar's own code and `data.token` the index of the offending token. | Fix the line; the grammar is in the Commands section. |
| <a id="err-position_not_attributable"></a>`position_not_attributable` | 403 | A key or an agent closes only the part of a position its own fills opened; this position (or that share of it) was opened by the owner or another principal. | Close only what the key opened; the owner closes anything. |
| <a id="err-position_notional_exceeded"></a>`position_notional_exceeded` | 422 | The resulting position exceeds the account policy for the instrument. | Send a smaller order. |
| <a id="err-price_deviation"></a>`price_deviation` | 422 | The price is further from the mark (or the trigger) than the account policy allows (`maxDeviationBps`; GET /risk shows the policy of each account). | Price the order closer to the mark. The mark is in GET /markets/{symbol}. |
| <a id="err-price_not_on_tick"></a>`price_not_on_tick` | 422 | The price is not a multiple of the instrument's `tickSize` (or has too many decimals for the venue). | Round the price to `tickSize` — as a decimal STRING, not a float. |
| <a id="err-price_required"></a>`price_required` | 422 | A limit order needs `price` (a market order needs a worst acceptable price, which the server derives). | Add `price`. |
| <a id="err-rate_limited"></a>`rate_limited` | 429 | More calls than the key's per-minute limit in the current one-minute window. | Wait `Retry-After` seconds (also `RateLimit-Reset`), then go on. Both SDKs do this for you. |
| <a id="err-read_only_mode"></a>`read_only_mode` | 423 | Read-only mode is on: only cancels pass (or: an agent in research mode places no order). | Cancel if needed; the owner releases it with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-reduce_only_would_flip"></a>`reduce_only_would_flip` | 422 | A reduce-only order is larger than the position. | Size it at most to the position; POST /positions/{id}/close does it for you. |
| <a id="err-reduce_only_would_increase"></a>`reduce_only_would_increase` | 422 | A reduce-only order would open or increase a position. | Check the side and the position. |
| <a id="err-restricted"></a>`restricted` | 403 | The key's restrictions (venues, instruments, networks) exclude what the call names. | Use a venue, instrument or network the key is allowed on, or have the owner widen the key (passkey). |
| <a id="err-risk_denied"></a>`risk_denied` | 422 | The independent risk engine refused the order. `data.failed` lists the failed check ids and `data.checks` gives, for each, what was observed and the limit; `data.order` is the order, state REJECTED. | Read `data.checks`. Frequent: a limit never configured on the live book, stale prices, an order too large for the collateral. See the risk checks table. |
| <a id="err-service_unavailable"></a>`service_unavailable` | 503 | No network / no position source could be read. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-signer_unreachable"></a>`signer_unreachable` | 503 | The isolated signer cannot be reached: balances, the portfolio and anything that needs a signature are unavailable. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. If it lasts, the owner checks the signer machine. |
| <a id="err-size_not_on_lot"></a>`size_not_on_lot` | 422 | The size is not a multiple of `lotSize`. | Round the size down to `lotSize`, or send `notional` and let the server size it. |
| <a id="err-spot_buy_needs_quote_amount"></a>`spot_buy_needs_quote_amount` | 422 | A swap is exact-input: a spot BUY names the amount of the quote asset to spend. | Send `notional` (not `size`) for a spot buy, or the command `BUY ETH 100 USDC`. |
| <a id="err-spot_execution_requires_agent_vault"></a>`spot_execution_requires_agent_vault` | 403 | An API key cannot place a spot order: spot execution for keys and agents exists only through an agent vault under a spot mandate. | Trade perpetuals with the key, or let a spot agent do it. |
| <a id="err-spot_market_only"></a>`spot_market_only` | 422 | A spot order here is an immediate swap bounded by `slippageBps`: resting and trigger orders do not exist for spot. | Send `type: "market"`. |
| <a id="err-spot_paper_not_available"></a>`spot_paper_not_available` | 422 | There is no paper spot engine for the owner. | Use mode `live` (a real swap, approved with the passkey); paper spot exists for agents only. |
| <a id="err-spot_via_swap_screen"></a>`spot_via_swap_screen` | 422 | An owner's spot order is a swap proposal approved with the passkey. | Prepare it in the Swap screen. |
| <a id="err-stale_book"></a>`stale_book` | 422 | The order book is older than the account policy allows. | Retry in a few seconds. |
| <a id="err-stale_data"></a>`stale_data` | 503 | The price or book needed is older than its freshness bound (e.g. a notional cannot be turned into a size at a stale mark). | Retry in a few seconds. A limit order sized with `size` names its own price and does not depend on the mark. |
| <a id="err-stale_request"></a>`stale_request` | 400 | `X-SPW-Timestamp` was sent and is more than 300 s from the server clock (or is not Unix seconds). | Fix the client clock, or do not send the header: it is optional. |
| <a id="err-stale_ticker"></a>`stale_ticker` | 422 | The venue ticker is older than the account policy allows (`maxDataAgeMs`). | Retry in a few seconds. |
| <a id="err-strategy_not_installed"></a>`strategy_not_installed` | 409 | No strategy of this template is installed in the laboratory. Nothing was created (no placeholder is substituted). | See `strategyInstalled` in GET /agents/templates. |
| <a id="err-swap_flow_failed"></a>`swap_flow_failed` | 502 | The swap flow returned no proposal. | Retry; if it lasts, prepare the swap in the app to see the reason. |
| <a id="err-swap_not_enabled"></a>`swap_not_enabled` | 422 | The wallet does not execute swaps on this network. | Use a network where swaps are enabled. |
| <a id="err-total_loss_exceeded"></a>`total_loss_exceeded` | 422 | The account's total loss limit is reached. | Reductions pass; Only the owner can change this, in the app, with the passkey. |
| <a id="err-trigger_required"></a>`trigger_required` | 422 | A stop or take-profit order needs `triggerPrice`. | Add `triggerPrice`. |
| <a id="err-unauthenticated"></a>`unauthenticated` | 401 | No credential, or a key that is malformed, unknown, wrong, expired or revoked. One answer for all of them, on purpose. | Check the `Authorization: Bearer spw_live_…` header and the key's status and expiry in Developer → Keys. Ten failures a minute from one address lead to `auth_throttled`. |
| <a id="err-universe_mismatch"></a>`universe_mismatch` | 422 | A fixed-universe strategy runs on its exact universe; the agent's differs. | Create the agent with the universe the message lists. |
| <a id="err-unknown_account"></a>`unknown_account` | 404 | No paper account / authorized venue account with this `accountId` on the venue. | Omit `accountId` when the wallet has one account on the venue; else take it from GET /portfolio (`accounts`). |
| <a id="err-unknown_company"></a>`unknown_company` | 404 | No reviewed private-company contract under this slug. | Take slugs from GET /private-markets (`companies`). |
| <a id="err-unknown_instrument"></a>`unknown_instrument` | 404, 422, 503 | 404: the venue does not list this instrument. 422: an instrument the order path cannot use (not in the pinned table of the venue). 503: the venue could not be read to find out. | Take ids from GET /markets (`venue:symbol`). On 503 retry later. |
| <a id="err-unknown_market"></a>`unknown_market` | 404 | A spot symbol without data, or a bare symbol nothing answers to. | Take the id from GET /markets. |
| <a id="err-unknown_network"></a>`unknown_network` | 404 | The network id is not one of the wallet's. | Take network ids from GET /balances. |
| <a id="err-unknown_nft"></a>`unknown_nft` | 404 | This NFT is not in the wallet's inventory. | Take `key` from GET /nfts. For any other item use GET /nfts/market. |
| <a id="err-unknown_order"></a>`unknown_order` | 404 | No order with this id (or none this key may see). | Take ids from GET /orders or from the answer of POST /orders. |
| <a id="err-unknown_position"></a>`unknown_position` | 404 | The position id is not of the form GET /positions returns, or names nothing. | Take `id` from GET /positions and URL-encode it. |
| <a id="err-unknown_private_market"></a>`unknown_private_market` | 404 | This id is not a private-company market known to the venues the wallet reads. | Take ids from GET /private-markets. |
| <a id="err-unknown_underlying"></a>`unknown_underlying` | 404 | No perpetual is mapped to this underlying in the comparator. | Use an underlying that GET /markets lists as a `base`. |
| <a id="err-unknown_venue"></a>`unknown_venue` | 404 | The venue id is not one this wallet knows (`hyperliquid`, `gmx`, `dydx`, `edgex`, `spot`). | List the ids with GET /markets. |
| <a id="err-unsupported_asset"></a>`unsupported_asset` | 422 | The base or quote asset is not exactly one asset of the wallet registry on that network. | Name assets the wallet lists in GET /balances. |
| <a id="err-unsupported_interval"></a>`unsupported_interval` | 422 | Spot market data does not store this candle interval. | Use one of the intervals the message lists. |
| <a id="err-venue_not_wired"></a>`venue_not_wired` | 422 | Live orders on this venue are not placed through this endpoint (GMX: prepared in the app, one passkey per order). This endpoint places live orders on Hyperliquid only. | Use `hyperliquid:*` for live orders through the API. |
| <a id="err-venue_rate_limited"></a>`venue_rate_limited` | 503 | The venue is rate-limiting this wallet. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-venue_rejected"></a>`venue_rejected` | 422 | The venue itself refused (the message quotes it): the order, a cancel, a margin mode it does not offer. | Read `message`; fix the order. |
| <a id="err-venue_restricted"></a>`venue_restricted` | 403 | The venue is never tradable from this wallet (dYdX and edgeX: market data only, a jurisdiction restriction). | Read its data; trade on Hyperliquid. |
| <a id="err-venue_timeout"></a>`venue_timeout` | 503 | The venue did not answer in time and no earlier value can stand in. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-venue_unavailable"></a>`venue_unavailable` | 503 | The venue (or the signer, for the wallet address) could not be read, so no honest answer exists. | Nothing was done. Retry with backoff; a mutating call keeps the same Idempotency-Key. |
| <a id="err-wrong_kind"></a>`wrong_kind` | 409 | The strategy and the agent are of different kinds (spot / perp), or the operation does not apply to this kind of agent. | Match the kinds. |
| <a id="err-zero_size"></a>`zero_size` | 422 | The quantity resolves to zero. | Raise the amount. |
